Result: 在 20 个表格-图像数据集上的实验表明，TI-Adapter 相比全量微调具有竞争性或更优的预测性能，同时可训练参数显著更少；消融实验验证了适配器放置位置对性能与效率平衡的重要性。

Conclusion: TI-Adapter 是一种有效的表格-图像多模态参数高效微调框架，能够在保持甚至提升预测性能的同时降低训练成本，并表明合理的适配器设计与放置对多模态适配至关重要。

Abstract: Tabular-image multimodal learning aims to improve predictive modeling by jointly using structured tabular attributes and visual data. Although pretrained encoders provide strong modality-specific representations, full fine-tuning can be computationally expensive, while keeping encoders frozen may limit task-specific adaptation. We propose the Tabular-Image Adapter (TI-Adapter), a modality-specific adapter-based fine-tuning framework for efficient multimodal adaptation. TI-Adapter freezes the pretrained tabular encoder and learns an adapter after the extracted tabular embedding, while adapting the image branch with embedding-level and bottleneck-level adapters instead of full fine-tuning. Experiments on 20 tabular-image datasets show that TI-Adapter achieves competitive or better predictive performance than full fine-tuning while using substantially fewer trainable parameters. Ablation studies further demonstrate the importance of adapter placement for balancing performance and practical efficiency.

</details>


### [37] [Reason, Then Re-reason: Cross-view Revisiting Improves Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11683)
*Chaofan Ma,Zhenjie Mao,Yuhuan Yang,Fanqin Zeng,Yue Shi,Yingjie Zhou,Xiaofeng Cao,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReRe：一种无需训练、在推理时先推理再复推理的空间推理框架，通过合成互补新视角视频来验证或修正初始空间假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频的空间推理受限于相机轨迹，单轮推理容易因可见证据不足而依赖语义先验，导致几何歧义难以被验证。作者认为空间结论应可被重新审视，并在获得互补视角后进行修正。

Method: 框架包含两个阶段：Reason Phase 中，多模态大语言模型基于原始视频形成空间假设；Re-reason Phase 中，模型观看由预测 3D 几何渲染出的新视角视频，对原假设进行验证或修订。为此设计 Geometry-to-Video 流水线，生成高位、斜视、覆盖全场景的互补视角，同时保持模型原生视频输入接口，无需改动架构或训练。

Result: 在 VSI-Bench 和 STI-Bench 上的评估显示，ReRe 显著提升开源多模态大语言模型的空间推理性能，使其接近或媲美专有模型的先进表现。

Conclusion: 通过在推理阶段引入可复查的跨视角证据，ReRe 有效缓解了自我中心视频中的空间歧义，证明了合成新视角视频可作为提升 MLLM 空间推理能力的实用训练-free策略。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning from egocentric videos is inherently challenging because the observable evidence is constrained by the camera trajectory. Existing methods rely on single-turn inference, forcing models to resolve geometric ambiguity through semantic priors rather than verifiable evidence. We argue that spatial reasoning should be revisitable: conclusions formed under limited evidence should remain open to revision when complementary viewpoints become available. Building on this insight, we propose Reason, then Re-reason (ReRe), a training-free, inference-time framework with two phases: in the Reason Phase, an MLLM forms a spatial hypothesis from the original video; in the Re-reason Phase, it verifies or revises the hypothesis by observing a synthesized novel-view video. To enable effective cross-view revisiting, we design a Geometry-to-Video pipeline that renders strategically complementary novel views from predicted 3D geometry. These views feature an elevated, oblique perspective with scene-spanning coverage, while preserving the MLLM's native video interface without architectural modifications. Extensive evaluations on VSI-Bench and STI-Bench demonstrate that ReRe substantially boosts open-source MLLMs to rival proprietary state-of-the-art performance. Project page: https://zhenjiemao.github.io/ReRe/

</details>

*Marius Bayizere*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) threats have emerged as a defining security challenge of the 21st century. This paper presents DroneShield-AI, a unified open framework integrating six processing layers: RF signal classification, acoustic motor-signature detection, YOLOv8-based visual detection, evidence-weighted sensor fusion, a Behavioral Intent Classification Engine (BICE), and a Graph Neural Network Swarm Intelligence Module (GNN-SIM). BICE introduces the first systematic six-class threat taxonomy for drone flight patterns, enabling predictive operator alerts with a 30-second advance-warning horizon. GNN-SIM is the first open framework for adversarial multi-drone formation analysis using Graph Attention Networks. Evaluated on three publicly available real-world datasets, the fused pipeline achieves 96.1% detection accuracy, 3.2% false alarm rate, AUC-ROC: 0.981, and 142ms end-to-end latency on commodity CPU-class hardware at approximately $500-$780 USD total system cost. All code, model weights, and simulation datasets are publicly released at submission.

### [39] [RankVR: Low-Rank Structure Perception and Value Recalibration for Robust Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11689)
*Jiale Huang,Zixu Li,Zhiheng Fu,Zhiwei Chen,Qinlei Huang,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RankVR，用于提升含噪三元组对应关系下的组合图像检索鲁棒性；通过全局结构一致性感知与自适应语义价值校准，区分结构噪声、保留高价值困难样本，并在 FashionIQ 与 CIRR 上取得优于现有方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合图像检索需要联合理解参考图像与修改文本，但大规模数据集中普遍存在 Noisy Triplet Correspondence，限制模型性能。已有去噪方法多关注二元错配或标量式逐点估计，忽略样本群体间的全局结构相关性以及训练过程中样本价值的动态变化，因此难以有效区分噪声与困难但有价值的干净样本。

Method: 提出 RankVR 框架，包含两个核心模块：1）Global Structure Consistency Perception（GSCP），利用相关矩阵的 Effective Rank 度量全局语义相关结构，通过 rank difference 识别破坏宏观语义对称性的结构噪声样本；2）Adaptive Semantic Value Calibration（ASVC），结合训练潜力与可靠性，动态量化每个三元组的语义价值，从而保留高价值困难干净样本并抑制存在逻辑冲突的噪声样本。

Result: 在 FashionIQ 和 CIRR 基准数据集上的大量实验表明，RankVR 显著优于现有最先进方法，尤其在噪声环境下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RankVR 通过全局结构一致性建模和动态语义价值感知，有效缓解组合图像检索中的噪声三元组问题，并提升了模型对困难样本与噪声样本的判别能力。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) constitutes a pivotal paradigm requiring models to perform joint reasoning on reference images and modification texts. However, the prevalence of Noisy Triplet Correspondence (NTC) in large-scale datasets severely constrains model performance. Existing denoising methods either target binary mismatches or rely on scalar-based point-wise estimation, neglecting rich global structural correlations among sample populations and dynamic value variations during training, thereby yielding suboptimal results. This paper identifies two critical unresolved challenges: Global Structural Inconsistency of Semantic Correlations and Hard Sample Discrimination Uncertainty. To address these, we propose RankVR, a framework designed to construct a robust CIR model via global structure consistency and dynamic value perception. Specifically, we introduce the Global Structure Consistency Perception (GSCP) module, which utilizes the Effective Rank of the Correlation Matrix to decouple clean samples from structural noise. By measuring rank difference, GSCP identifies samples disrupting macroscopic semantic symmetry. Furthermore, we develop the Adaptive Semantic Value Calibration (ASVC) module to distinguish high-value hard clean samples. By integrating training potential and reliability, it dynamically quantifies the semantic value of each triplet, ensuring effective utilization of hard samples while suppressing noise characterized by logical conflicts. Extensive experiments on the FashionIQ and CIRR benchmark datasets demonstrate that RankVR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, validating its superior robustness in noisy environments.

</details>


### [40] [MedCTA: A Benchmark for Clinical Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11702)
*Tajamul Ashraf,Hyewon Jeong,Fida Mohammad Thoker,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedCTA提出了一个面向医疗多模态工具智能体的基准，用于评估其在真实临床任务中的多步工具使用能力；实验显示当前前沿模型在规划、工具选择与稳定执行方面仍然脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准多关注单一感知任务或单轮问答，难以揭示模型在临床工具调用场景中的规划失败、工具选择错误、执行不稳定和多步推理缺陷。临床决策需要AI能够检索工具、获取证据并整合结果，因此需要更贴近真实临床流程的评测体系。

Method: 作者构建MedCTA基准，包含107个真实临床任务，覆盖放射影像、病理切片和报告等多模态输入。每个任务配有临床专家验证的可执行工具调用轨迹，涉及5个已部署工具。评测维度包括工具选择、参数有效性、执行稳定性、轨迹一致性和最终结果质量，并对18个开源与闭源多模态模型进行基准测试。

Result: 实验发现，即使是前沿多模态模型，在自主多步临床工具使用中仍表现脆弱，常见问题包括流程协议失败、过早停止和错误工具调用。若提供金标准工具路由，性能显著提升，但仍不能完全解决问题。

Conclusion: 强大的基础视觉或语言感知能力并不自动转化为可靠的临床智能体行为。MedCTA为审计、诊断和改进可信医疗AI工具智能体提供了严格测试平台。

Abstract: To make clinically grounded decisions, medical AI agents are expected to go beyond simple recognition and be capable of tool retrieval, evidence acquisition, and integration. Existing benchmarks largely evaluate isolated perception or single-turn question answering, and therefore provide limited visibility into failures of planning, tool recruitment, and rollout reliability. We introduce MedCTA, a benchmark for evaluating medical tool agents on clinician-validated, step-implicit tasks grounded in realistic multimodal clinical inputs, including radiology images, pathology slides, and reports. MedCTA comprises 107 real-world clinical tasks with clinician-verified executable trajectories over 5 deployed tools, and supports process-aware evaluation of tool selection, argument validity, execution stability, trajectory fidelity, and outcome quality. We benchmark 18 open- and closed-source multimodal models and find that even frontier systems remain brittle in multi-step clinical tool use: autonomous rollouts are dominated by protocol failures, premature stopping, and incorrect tool recruitment, while gold-standard tool routing yields large but still incomplete gains. These results show that strong backbone perception does not translate into reliable agentic behavior in clinical settings. MedCTA provides a rigorous testbed for auditing, diagnosing, and advancing trustworthy medical AI agents. The dataset and evaluation suite are available at https://ivul-kaust.github.io/MedCTA/

</details>


### [41] [ERN-Net : Evolving Reason Node-Net for Document Binarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11710)
*Hsin-Jui Pan,Sheng-Wei Chan,Jen-Shiung Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ERN-Net通过演化推理节点与多尺度推理增强文档图像二值化中的退化敏感区域；ConvNeXt-Tiny在精度与显存之间最均衡，DIBCO预训练可在几乎不增加推理/模型内存的情况下提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档图像二值化在低质量扫描、淡笔画、断裂字符和噪声背景等场景中仍具挑战，且实际部署常受限于小数据集与低显存环境，因此需要兼顾精度、鲁棒性和资源效率的方法。

Method: 提出ERN-Net（Evolving Reason Node-Net），利用演化推理节点和多尺度推理机制对退化敏感区域进行增强；比较ResNet-101、ConvNeXt-Tiny与ConvNeXt-Base作为骨干网络；引入基于DIBCO数据的预训练策略以提升二值化表现。

Result: 实验显示，ConvNeXt-Tiny在准确率和内存占用之间提供最佳实用折中；DIBCO预训练能提升二值化性能，不增加模型内存消耗，仅额外需要约1.5小时训练；ERN-Net在DIBCO风格基准上适用于低数据、低内存设置。

Conclusion: ERN-Net是一种面向资源受限场景的高效文档图像二值化方法，通过区域增强、多尺度推理和轻量化骨干选择提升了退化文档处理性能，具有较好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents ERN-Net, an Evolving Reason Node-Net for efficient document image binarization. ERN-Net enhances degradation-sensitive regions, such as faint strokes, broken characters, and noisy backgrounds, through evolving reason nodes and multi-scale reasoning. We further compare ResNet-101, ConvNeXt-Tiny, and ConvNeXt-Base, and find that ConvNeXt-Tiny provides the best practical trade-off between accuracy and memory usage. In addition, DIBCO-based pretraining improves binarization performance without increasing model memory consumption, requiring only about 1.5 additional training hours. Experiments on DIBCO-style benchmarks show that ERN-Net is effective under low-data and low-memory settings.

</details>


### [42] [Ouroboros-Spatial: Closing the Data-Model Loop for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11719)
*Enhan Zhao,Wei Wu,Yuanrui Zhang,Xueliang Zhao,Di He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Ouroboros-Spatial，一个用于提升多模态大语言模型空间推理能力的自进化训练框架，通过“出题者-解题者”闭环动态生成适配模型能力的训练样本，以更少数据取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 空间推理训练多依赖大规模静态数据集，所有样本被同等对待，无法随模型能力变化调整训练难度，导致大量训练资源浪费在过于简单或过难的样本上，数据效率较低。

Method: 框架中模型扮演双重角色：冻结的 proposer 基于 3D 场景元数据和原始视频帧生成空间问答样本，并提供可执行代码以获得可靠真值；可学习的 solver 在筛选后的样本上微调，并用逐样本预测置信度作为难度信号。该信号反馈给下一轮 proposer，引导其生成更符合当前 solver 能力的问题。

Result: 在六个空间推理基准上，Ouroboros-Spatial 显著提升 Qwen3-VL-4B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B，且训练样本量比近期大规模人工/静态数据集少一个数量级。在 VSI-Bench 上，4B 和 8B 模型分别获得 9.9 和 6.8 个绝对点提升，并超过多种强开源和闭源基线。

Conclusion: 动态闭环式自进化数据生成能够让训练分布与模型能力共同演化，减少冗余简单样本并过滤模糊或低价值样本，从而以更高数据效率增强 MLLM 的空间推理能力。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning remains a persistent challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing approaches largely rely on large-scale, statically curated datasets, where all training samples are treated uniformly regardless of the model's evolving capabilities. This static paradigm is inherently data-inefficient: training capacity is often spent on samples that are either trivial or overly difficult for the model at its current stage. To address this limitation, we propose Ouroboros-Spatial, a self-evolving training framework in which the model plays dual roles as a proposer and a solver. In each iteration, a frozen proposer generates spatial question-answer (QA) pairs from 3D scene metadata and raw video frames, together with executable code for deriving reliable ground truth. A learnable solver is then fine-tuned on the accepted samples, and its per-sample prediction confidence is used as a difficulty signal. This signal is fed back to the proposer in the next iteration, guiding it to generate questions better matched to the solver's current capabilities. Through this closed-loop design, the training distribution co-evolves with model ability, reducing redundant trivial examples while filtering out ambiguous or uninformative samples with limited learning value. Across six spatial reasoning benchmarks, Ouroboros-Spatial substantially improves Qwen3-VL-4B and Qwen3-VL-8B while using an order of magnitude fewer training examples than recent large-scale curated datasets. On VSI-Bench, it yields absolute gains of 9.9 and 6.8 points for the 4B and 8B models, respectively, enabling both to outperform a wide range of strong open-source and proprietary baselines.

</details>


### [43] [Multi-View In-Cabin Monitoring System for Public Transport Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11739)
*Evgeny Gorelik,Kenny Dean Karrow,Fikret Sivrikaya,Sahin Albayrak,Christian Baumann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个面向公共交通车厢内部感知的多视角数据集，包含同步RGB、深度图像和LiDAR数据，并提供标注、校准、伪标签生成、nuScenes格式转换及基准模型评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车厢内乘员感知对数字化与部分自动化公共交通具有重要意义，但现有公开数据集较少覆盖车内多视角、多模态、同步传感器数据，难以支持3D检测、姿态估计和占用理解等任务的系统评估。

Method: 作者在一辆数字化、部分自动化的德国城市公交车内部布设四个朝内相机采集同步RGB与深度图像，并使用旋转LiDAR覆盖车厢内部；构建包含9,136个同步样本的数据集，提供标注信息，同时设计校准与伪标签流程以生成乘员3D人体姿态估计和带方向的3D边界框，并将数据转换为nuScenes格式以适配现有多视角3D检测框架。

Result: 论文提供了数据集、工具链和开源仓库，并基于代表性多视角3D检测模型（如Lift-Splat-Shoot和BEVFusion）进行了基准测试，展示该数据集可用于多视角车厢内感知模型的比较评估和小规模训练。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了公共交通车厢内部多视角、多模态3D感知数据资源的空白，为车内乘员检测、姿态估计和多视角BEV感知研究提供了可复现的数据与评测基础。

Abstract: We introduce a multi-view in-cabin monitoring dataset for public transportation with synchronized RGB and depth images from four inward-facing cameras and a rotating LiDAR covering the vehicle interior of a digitalized and partly automated German city bus. The dataset contains 9.136 synchronized samples with annotations and is accompanied by a calibration and pseudo-labeling pipeline that generates 3D human pose estimates and oriented 3D bounding boxes for occupants. We further provide a nuScenes-format conversion and benchmark representative multi-view 3D detection models (e.g., Lift-Splat-Shoot and BEVFusion), supporting comparative evaluation and small-scale training of multi-view in-cabin perception models. The dataset and tools are available at https://github.com/EvgenyGorelik/multiview_incabin_dataset.

</details>


### [44] [UniReason-Med: A Shared Grounded Reasoning Interface for 2D-to-3D Transfer in Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11740)
*Mengzhuo Chen,Yan Shu,Chi Liu,Hongming Piao,Xidong Wang,Derek Li,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 UniReason-Med，通过统一的接地推理接口将丰富的 2D 医学图像监督迁移到 3D 医学 VQA，显著提升 3D 推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 医学 VQA 数据与监督相对稀缺，而 2D 医学图像资源更丰富。论文希望探索：若 2D 图像与 3D 体数据通过统一的推理与视觉证据表示对齐，2D 中学到的接地推理能力是否能迁移并增强 3D 医学视觉问答。

Method: 作者提出 UniReason-Med 单检查点框架，可在推理时处理 2D 图像或切片序列化的 3D 体数据。模型生成交错的文本推理与局部视觉证据，并通过共享框语法、区域 token 注入和统一接地推理策略实现 2D/3D 对齐。训练方面构建 UniMed-CoT 数据集，包含 220K 指令微调样本，其中 170K 为 2D、50K 为 3D，均带有文本推理和视觉证据。训练流程包括监督微调以及结果级强化学习，且强化学习阶段不依赖 IoU/Dice 等定位奖励。

Result: 实验中的数据混合与组件消融表明，联合使用 2D+3D 接地监督相比仅使用 3D 训练能显著提升 3D 推理表现；同时，视觉接地机制和区域 token 注入对 2D 与 3D 任务均有稳定收益。

Conclusion: 研究证明，共享的接地推理接口能够促进从 2D 医学图像到切片序列化 3D 医学理解的推理结构迁移，为低资源 3D 医学 VQA 提供了一种有效训练范式。

Abstract: We study whether grounded reasoning supervision from abundant 2D medical images can improve 3D medical VQA when both input types are aligned through a common reasoning interface. We introduce UniReason-Med, a single-checkpoint framework that processes either a 2D image or a slice-serialized 3D volume at inference time, generating interleaved textual reasoning and localized visual evidence through shared box syntax, region-token injection, and a common grounded reasoning policy. To train this interface, we construct UniMed-CoT, a 220K instruction-tuning dataset with interleaved textual reasoning and grounded visual evidence, including 170K 2D and 50K 3D samples. Through supervised fine-tuning followed by outcome-level reinforcement learning, UniReason-Med learns to generate grounded reasoning traces without IoU/Dice-based localization rewards during RL. Data-mixture and component ablations show that joint 2D+3D grounded supervision substantially improves 3D reasoning over 3D-only training, while grounding and region-token injection consistently benefit both 2D and 3D tasks. These results suggest that a shared grounded reasoning interface can transfer reasoning structure from 2D images to slice-serialized volumetric medical understanding. The code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/IQuestLab/unireason-med.

</details>


### [45] [From Prompts to Tokens: Internalizing Causal Supervision in Vision-Language Model for Multi-Image Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11745)
*Haoping Yu,Yuanxi Li,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BridgeVLM通过从多图像输入中诱导因果图，并将其转化为可在LLM解码器中执行的结构化因果Token，从而提升视觉因果推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉语言模型在视觉因果推理，尤其是多图像输入下的干预和反事实查询中表现脆弱；多数方法依赖文本提示注入因果知识，使因果机制停留在模型执行之外，限制了推理时的可靠控制。

Method: 提出BridgeVLM：先从多图像输入中归纳视觉因果图，再将其转换为结构化Causal Tokens，并通过注入LLM解码器的RAMP层进行因果消息传递；同时提出统一训练接口M3S，以支持局部与全局不同粒度的细粒度因果监督。

Result: 在CausalVLBench干预任务上达到54.4%准确率，优于提示级监督的33.2%；在Causal3D上从43.6%提升到49.0%；在CausalVLBench因果结构学习中F1从33.4%提升到75.1%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将视觉因果结构内化为模型执行过程中的显式结构表示与消息传递机制，比仅依赖文本提示更能提升VLM的干预推理与因果结构学习能力。

Abstract: Visual causal reasoning is essential for understanding and intervening in the physical world, requiring identification of causal variables from visual inputs and reasoning over intervention effects. Despite recent progress, large vision--language models (VLMs) remain brittle at such tasks, especially for interventional and counterfactual queries over multi-image inputs. Most existing explorations inject causal knowledge via textual prompts, leaving causal mechanisms external to model execution and limiting reliable control during inference. To address this problem, we propose BridgeVLM, which internalizes visual causal reasoning by inducing a causal graph from multi-image inputs and converting it into structured Causal Tokens executed by RAMP layers injected into the LLM decoder for causal message passing. We further introduce a unified training interface M3S for fine-grained causal supervision from different granularities (local/global level). BridgeVLM achieves 54.4% accuracy on intervention tasks on CausalVLBench (vs. 33.2% with prompt-level supervision), improves results on Causal3D from 43.6% to 49.0%, and substantially improves causal structure learning on CausalVLBench ($F_1$: 33.4% $\rightarrow$ 75.1%).

</details>


### [46] [AnchorEdit: Maintaining Temporal Consistency in Multi-turn Image Editing via Causal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11751)
*Hang Xu,Xiaoxiao Ma,Guohui Zhang,Yu Hu,Siming Fu,Jie Huang,Lin Song,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorEdit 是一种面向高分辨率、长程多轮图像编辑的自回归扩散框架，通过训练课程与记忆机制缓解身份漂移和误差累积，在 10 轮以上交互中取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮图像编辑需要在连续指令下保持主体一致性和编辑可控性，但现有模型在多步编辑中容易出现身份漂移、误差累积。既有方法虽借助视频先验提升一致性，但常依赖双向注意力，与交互式编辑的因果、顺序推理过程不匹配。

Method: 论文提出 AnchorEdit：首个专为高分辨率长期多轮编辑设计的自回归扩散框架。方法包括三阶段训练：1）保持身份的单轮编辑预训练；2）因果自回归强制微调，并使用自回滚策略缓解 exposure bias；3）一致性蒸馏以实现高效 4 步生成。推理阶段引入记忆机制，锚定初始主体身份并支持长编辑轨迹中的稳定外推。

Result: 作者构建新的高分辨率多轮编辑基准，用于检验长程稳定性。实验显示 AnchorEdit 达到当前最优效果，在 10 轮以上交互中仍能保持较高主体保真度和指令遵循能力。

Conclusion: AnchorEdit 将视频一致性先验与因果自回归推理相结合，有效解决多轮图像编辑中的长期一致性问题，为高分辨率交互式图像编辑提供了更稳定、高效的方案。

Abstract: Multi-turn image editing is essential for iterative design, yet current models often struggle with identity drift and error accumulation over successive steps. While existing research leverages video priors for consistency, their reliance on bidirectional attention is fundamentally misaligned with the causal, sequential nature of interactive editing. In this paper, we propose AnchorEdit, the first autoregressive (AR) diffusion-based framework designed specifically for high-resolution, long-term multi-turn editing. AnchorEdit bridges the gap between video priors and causal inference through a three-stage training curriculum: identity-preserving sing-turn pretraining, causal AR forcing fine-tuning with a novel self-rollout strategy to mitigate exposure bias, and consistency distillation for efficient 4-step generation. During inference, we introduce a memory mechanism to anchor the initial subject identity and ensure stable extrapolation across extended editing trajectories. To evaluate performance, we provide a new high-resolution multi-turn editing benchmark designed to stress-test long-horizon stability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AnchorEdit achieves state-of-the-art results, maintaining exceptional subject fidelity and instruction following even over 10+ interaction rounds.

</details>


### [47] [Battery detection of XRay images using transfer learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11779)
*Nermeen Abou Baker,David Rohrschneider,Uwe Handmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究利用迁移学习和 YOLOv5m 实现锂离子电池图像检测、定位与类型识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池检测与分拣需求快速增长，需要高效、准确的视觉方法判断图像中是否存在电池、定位电池并区分不同电池形态。

Method: 先使用预训练 YOLOv5m 在大规模电子设备数据集上训练检测电子设备，再将获得的权重迁移到电池检测与分类任务中，用于识别棱柱形、软包形和圆柱形三类锂离子电池。

Result: 所提出迁移学习方案的电池检测精度达到 94%，相比直接使用预训练 YOLOv5m 权重提升 5%，推理时间为 22 ms。

Conclusion: 基于 YOLOv5m 的分阶段迁移学习在电池检测、定位与分类中具有较好潜力，可支持快速自动化电池分拣应用。

Abstract: The need for detecting and sorting batteries is drastically increasing for many applications. This study proves the potential of transfer learning in predicting whether the image contains a battery or not, the location and identifying three types of batteries, namely: prismatic, pouch, and cylindrical Lithium-Ion Batteries (LIB). Particularly, it focuses on the transfer learning method in two applications: Training a large-scale dataset to detect electronic devices using a pre-trained YOLOv5m, then using these latter trained weights to detect and classify the batteries. The precision of battery detection achieves 94%, which outperforms the pretrained YOLOv5m weights with 5%, in 22 ms inference time.

</details>


### [48] [Seeing What Matters: Perceptual Wrapper with Common Randomness for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11782)
*He-Bi Yang,Jing-Zhong Chen,Yen-Kuan Ho,Sang NguyenQuang,Fan-Yi Hsu,Yun-Yu Lee,Jui-Chiu Chiang,Wen-Hsiao Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种即插即用的二维感知增强包装器，用于提升3D Gaussian Splatting渲染结果中的高频纹理表现，尤其适用于内存受限和率失真优化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能实现实时渲染，但在合成高频纹理时常出现模糊问题；在压缩、内存受限或RDO优化管线中，这一问题更加明显，因此需要一种不依赖重建底层3D表示、可广泛适配现有方法的感知质量增强方案。

Method: 作者设计了一个轻量级二维合成网络，作为现有3DGS渲染输出后的感知增强模块。该网络根据内容和视角自适应工作，并以伪随机高斯噪声为条件生成感知上合理的纹理细节。训练时采用Wasserstein Distortion进行监督，使模型匹配局部特征统计，而非严格追求逐像素一致性，从而减少传统重建损失导致的模糊。

Result: 实验表明，该方法可应用于原始3DGS、内存受限3DGS以及RDO 3DGS等多类框架。在主观和客观评测中，相比现有基线均显著提升感知质量，并能在更小文件或模型规模下获得更好的视觉效果。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，通过二维后处理式感知增强，可以有效弥补3DGS在高频纹理重建上的不足；其轻量、即插即用和跨框架适配特性使其适合用于压缩或资源受限的3DGS渲染系统。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves impressive real-time rendering, it frequently struggles to synthesize high-frequency textures, a limitation heavily exacerbated in memory-constrained and rate-distortion-optimized (RDO) pipelines. To address this, we propose a versatile 2D perceptual wrapper that enhances the rendered outputs of existing 3DGS representations in a content- and view-dependent manner. Our method leverages a lightweight synthesis network conditioned on pseudo-random Gaussian noise to synthesize perceptually plausible textures. Supervised by Wasserstein Distortion, the network learns to match local feature statistics rather than strictly enforcing pixel-wise reconstruction fidelity, effectively mitigating the blurriness inherent in standard frameworks. We demonstrate the broad applicability of our plug-and-play approach across vanilla, memory-constrained, and RDO 3DGS methods. Comprehensive subjective and objective experiments confirm that our method significantly improves over existing baselines, yielding superior perceptual quality at sharply reduced file or model sizes.

</details>


### [49] [A Comprehensive Ecosystem for Open-Domain Customized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11783)
*Jingxu Zhang,Yuqian Hong,Daneul Kim,Kai Qiu,Qi Dai,Jianmin Bao,Yifan Yang,Xiaoyan Sun,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向身份保持视频生成的百万级数据集 PexelsCustom-1M、参数高效模型 CustoMDiT，以及更大规模评测基准 OpenCustom，用于推动开放域定制化视频生成研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成虽具备较强视觉合成能力，但开放域定制化视频生成受限于缺乏大规模、带标注且覆盖多样身份属性的数据集；同时既有基准类别规模较小，难以反映真实应用需求。

Method: 构建包含一百万个 <identity, text, video> 三元组、覆盖 8000+ 类别的 PexelsCustom-1M 数据集；提出 CustoMDiT，以仅增加 8% 可学习参数的方式，将预训练多模态 Diffusion Transformer 适配为定制化视频生成器；并通过 ImageNet 与 MS-COCO 的跨数据集知识融合构建覆盖 1000+ 类别的 OpenCustom 基准。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的数据集和模型均带来明显优势，CustoMDiT 超越此前最先进方法；OpenCustom 相比 DreamBooth 等仅覆盖约 100 类的基准更适合评估真实开放域场景。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性补齐了开放域身份保持视频生成在数据、模型和评测方面的不足，并计划开源数据集、构建流程、基准和实现，以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Recent progress in video generation has shown impressive visual synthesis capabilities. However, open-domain customized video generation remains limited by the lack of large-scale, annotated datasets capturing diverse identity-specific attributes. To address this, we introduce PexelsCustom-1M, the first publicly available million-scale dataset for identity-preserving video generation, containing one million curated <identity, text, video> triplets across 8,000+ categories. Leveraging this, we propose CustoMDiT, a parameter-efficient framework that adapts a pretrained multimodal Diffusion Transformer into a customized video generator with only 8% additional learnable parameters. Our method surpasses prior state-of-the-art. However, benchmarks such as DreamBooth cover only 100 classes, which is insufficient for real-world applications. To overcome this, we construct OpenCustom, a new benchmark with 1,000+ categories, created via cross-dataset knowledge fusion from ImageNet and MS-COCO. Extensive experiments confirm the advantages of both our dataset and model. We will open-source the entire ecosystem--including dataset, pipeline, benchmark, and implementations--to support further research.

</details>


### [50] [MultiToP: Learning to Patch Visual Tokens to Mitigate Hallucinations in Video Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11792)
*Yuansheng Gao,Wenbin Xing,Jiahao Yuan,Kaiwen Zhou,Han Bao,Zonghui Wang,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MultiToP：一种在语言生成前修补不可靠视觉 token 的轻量框架，用于降低视频大多模态模型幻觉，同时基本不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大多模态模型在视频理解中表现突出，但生成内容可能与输入视频证据不一致，产生幻觉。现有方法需要在不改动原模型、低开销的前提下提升视觉证据可靠性。

Method: MultiToP 引入 Visual Token Patcher，预测 token 级替换分布，并将不可靠视觉 token 选择性替换为动态全局 patch token；同时提出信息引导的排序校准，利用骨干模型产生的答案条件帧级信息线索指导 token 替换，并结合真实答案监督与稀疏正则进行训练。

Result: 在 Vript-HAL 上显著降低幻觉，Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct 的 F1 相比原始模型提升 50.60%；推理开销可忽略。与此同时保持通用视频理解能力，在 ActivityNet-QA 上使 Video-LLaVA-7B 相对准确率提升 18.58%。

Conclusion: MultiToP 通过局部化视觉证据精炼有效缓解视频多模态模型幻觉，并能在不修改原模型的情况下兼顾低成本部署和通用视频理解性能。

Abstract: Video Large Multimodal Models have achieved remarkable progress in video understanding, yet they remain prone to hallucinations, where generated responses are not faithfully supported by the input video. In this paper, we propose MultiToP, a multimodal-context-aware visual token patching framework that mitigates hallucinations by refining unreliable visual tokens before language generation. MultiToP introduces a lightweight Visual Token Patcher to predict token-level replacement distributions and selectively substitute unreliable visual tokens with a dynamic global patch token. To train the patcher effectively, we further propose information-guided rank calibration, which uses answer-conditioned frame-level information cues derived from the backbone to guide token replacement. Combined with ground-truth answer supervision and sparsity regularization, MultiToP enables localized visual evidence refinement without modifying the original model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MultiToP effectively reduces hallucinations on Vript-HAL with negligible inference overhead, improving the F1 scores of Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct by 50.60% over the vanilla model. Meanwhile, MultiToP preserves general video understanding ability, yielding an 18.58% relative accuracy gain on ActivityNet-QA for Video-LLaVA-7B.

</details>


### [51] [TextHOI-3D: Text-to-3D Hand-Object Interaction via Discrete Multi-View Generation and Joint Mesh Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11805)
*Zixiong Hao,Zhencun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextHOI-3D 通过“文本生成多视角视觉token—几何恢复统一手-物网格”的分阶段框架，实现更一致、更低穿透的文本条件3D手物交互生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件3D生成在图像或孤立物体方面进展较快，但生成手-物交互网格仍困难，需要同时满足语言语义一致性、跨视角一致性、物体几何合理性、关节化手部形状以及物理接触可信性。

Method: 提出 TextHOI-3D：先学习固定相机手-物观测的紧凑VQ离散token空间；再用CLIP条件的视觉自回归模型从文本预测多视角视觉token；最后通过先验初始化、多视角联合优化和抗穿透细化，恢复统一的手-物3D网格。该方法将语义生成与几何恢复解耦，并用离散多视角表示连接两阶段。

Result: 在HO3D衍生评测中，相比单视角方案，多视角设置将物体Chamfer Distance从17.26 mm降至4.92 mm，穿透体积从5.3721 cm³降至0.2193 cm³，同时改善手部误差和表面F-score。

Conclusion: 多视角视觉token是文本驱动3D手-物网格生成的有效中间表示，能够提升几何一致性、接触合理性与整体重建质量。

Abstract: Text-conditioned 3D generation has progressed rapidly for images and isolated objects, but producing a hand-object mesh remains challenging: the output must preserve language semantics, cross-view consistency, object geometry, articulated hand shape, and physically plausible contact. We present TextHOI-3D, a staged framework that uses generated multi-view observations as an explicit interface between text-conditioned visual generation and geometry-aware hand-object recovery. TextHOI-3D learns a compact VQ token space for fixed-camera hand-object observations, predicts multi-view visual tokens from text with a CLIP-conditioned visual autoregressive model, and recovers a unified hand-object mesh through prior initialization, multi-view joint optimization, and anti-penetration refinement. The design separates semantic generation from geometric recovery while keeping both stages connected by a discrete multi-view representation. On HO3D-derived evaluations, the multi-view setting reduces object CD from 17.26 mm to 4.92 mm and penetration volume from 5.3721 cm^3 to 0.2193 cm^3 compared with a single-view counterpart, while improving hand errors and surface F-scores. These results support multi-view visual tokens as an effective intermediate representation for text-driven 3D hand-object mesh creation.

</details>

### [52] [PoQ-Judge: A Multi-Architecture Evaluation Framework for Cost-Aware Proof-of-Quality in Decentralized LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11196)
*Arther Tian,Alex Ding,Frank Chen,Simon Wu,Aaron Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 PoQ-Judge：用于去中心化 LLM 推理网络中 Proof of Quality 的轻量级、无参考质量评估框架；最佳模型在测试集上达到 0.747 Pearson 相关，并可通过级联评估显著降本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化 LLM 推理网络需要在没有标准答案或参考答案的情况下，对查询-输出质量进行低成本、可扩展、可靠的评估，以支撑 Proof of Quality。传统参考式评估依赖参考答案，成本和适用性受限。

Method: 训练专用 judge 模型对 query-output pair 进行无参考打分。比较 TextCNN、MiniLM cross-encoder、DeBERTa 三类架构，并采用两阶段训练：先用 UltraFeedback，再用 GPT 标注的领域内数据。还研究在线校准、复合评分中的无参考组件，以及级联评估以降低推理成本。

Result: 最佳模型在 held-out 测试集上与 ground-truth proxy 达到 0.747 Pearson 相关，超过既有参考式评估器。作为复合评分中的无参考组件达到 0.645 Pearson 相关，与最佳单一参考式评估器相当。在线校准显示语义质量是主导维度；级联评估降低 72.7% 成本，仅带来有限质量损失。QA 任务表现显著强于摘要任务。

Conclusion: PoQ-Judge 证明了无参考轻量评估可用于去中心化 LLM 推理网络的质量证明，并能在质量与成本间取得良好平衡；但其效果受 ground-truth proxy/标注代理质量限制，尤其在摘要类任务上仍需改进。

Abstract: Decentralized LLM inference networks need lightweight, reference-free quality evaluation for Proof of Quality (PoQ). We present PoQ-Judge, a framework that trains dedicated judge models to score query-output pairs without ground-truth references. We study three architectures across the quality-cost tradeoff: a TextCNN judge, a MiniLM cross-encoder, and a DeBERTa judge. Using two-stage training on UltraFeedback plus GPT-labeled in-domain data, the best model reaches 0.747 Pearson correlation with the ground-truth proxy on a held-out test set, outperforming reference-based evaluators from prior work. As a reference-free component in composite scoring, it achieves 0.645 Pearson correlation, matching the best single reference-based evaluator while removing the need for reference answers. We also show that online calibration identifies semantic quality as the dominant dimension and that cascade evaluation reduces cost by 72.7 percent with only modest quality loss. Results are much stronger on QA than summarization, pointing to proxy quality as the main remaining limitation.

</details>


### [53] [The Structural Attention Tax: How Retrieval Format Hijacks In-Context Learning Independent of Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11198)
*Yuqi Zhang,Di Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出“结构性注意力税”：RAG 中注入内容的格式会独立于语义相关性扭曲 LLM 注意力，KG 三元组因结构模式吸引过多注意力，压缩示例注意力并影响 ICL 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG/ICL 研究多关注检索内容的语义相关性，但忽视注入知识的表示格式本身可能改变模型注意力分配。作者希望解释为什么语义等价但结构不同的知识输入会导致不同效果，并为改进检索增强 ICL 提供理论依据。

Method: 作者形式化定义结构性注意力税，将注意力分数分解为语义项与结构项；推导 token 级格式偏置与示例注意力损失之间的压缩界；在 Mistral-7B、LLaMA-3-8B 及三个 QA 基准上比较 KG 三元组、自然语言文本、BM25 检索、ConceptNet 等设置，并提出五类结构感知缓解策略。

Result: KG 三元组每 token 获得的注意力约为自然语言中性文本的 2–3 倍，且最多压缩 demonstration attention 42%，不论三元组是否相关。实验显示源任务对齐更关键：任务匹配的 BM25 在 HotpotQA 上达 58–62%，ConceptNet 仅 25–27%，差距超过 30 个百分点，而各类 gating 策略提升不超过 2 个百分点。格式扁平化策略获得准确率与注意力证据支持，结构分散策略结果不稳定。

Conclusion: RAG/ICL 的改进应同时考虑语义检索质量与结构格式偏置两个正交维度。结构项决定注意力被分流多少，语义项决定这种分流是有益还是有害；因此，仅优化检索相关性不足，还需降低格式驱动的注意力捕获。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems inject external knowledge to improve LLM outputs, yet the format of injected content -- distinct from its semantic relevance -- can independently distort the model's attention distribution. We identify and formalise a phenomenon we term the structural attention tax: knowledge graph (KG) triples, due to their relational delimiters and repeated slot patterns, capture 2-3x more attention per token than semantically equivalent natural-language text ($\hat{o}$(KG) $\approx$ 0.70 vs. $\hat{o}$(neutral) $\approx$ 0.25), compressing demonstration attention by up to 42% -- regardless of whether the triples are relevant or noise. We develop a formal framework decomposing attention scores into semantic and structural components (Eq. 2), derive a compression bound (Proposition 1) connecting token-level format bias to demonstration attention loss, and show that the structural term governs how much attention is diverted while the semantic term governs whether this helps or hurts. This decoupling reveals two orthogonal axes for improving retrieval-augmented ICL: optimising retrieval quality (semantic axis) and reducing format-driven attention capture (structural axis). Empirically, across two model families (Mistral-7B, LLaMA-3-8B) and three QA benchmarks, we observe that source-task alignment dominates: task-matched BM25 retrieval achieves 58-62% on HotpotQA vs. ConceptNet's 25-27%, a >30 pp gap that dwarfs all gating strategies ($\leq$2 pp). We derive five structure-aware mitigation strategies from the framework, ranging from zero-cost prompt modifications to training-time regularisation; format flattening (S3) is validated by both accuracy and attention-level evidence from a verbalized-triple control, while structural dispersal (S1) yields mixed results that illuminate the challenges of format-level intervention.

</details>


### [54] [NightFeats @ MMU-RAGent NeurIPS 2025: A Context-Optimized Multi-Agent RAG System for the Text-to-Text Track](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11199)
*Quentin Fever,Naziha Aslam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NightFeats 是一个结构化多智能体 RAG 系统，通过检索、策展与生成三阶段流水线提升知识综合的可解释性、证据可验证性与人类偏好一致性，并在 MMU-RAGent 竞赛文本到文本赛道动态评测中获奖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG/智能体系统往往过度追求基准分数或自动相似度指标，缺乏透明的中间表示、可靠的证据 grounding 以及面向人类偏好的知识综合机制。本文希望构建一种更原则化、可审计、可验证的 RAG 架构。

Method: 提出 NightFeats：一个受 Agentic Context Engineering 启发的结构化多智能体 RAG 管线。系统将任务分解为检索、策展、组合三个阶段，并通过显式中间表示和阶段间 handoff contracts 协调。核心机制包括时间-语义重排序、有界矛盾调和以及保留引用的答案组合。

Result: 在 NeurIPS 2025 MMU-RAGent 竞赛文本到文本赛道中获得 Best Dynamic Evaluation。实验/竞赛结果显示，其在 LLM-as-a-Judge 和 Human Likert 评估上超过 Claude-SonnetV2 与 Nova-Pro 等专有基线。

Conclusion: 论文认为，具备架构透明性和可验证证据 grounding 的 RAG 系统，比单纯针对自动相似度指标优化的系统更符合人类偏好，并能在动态评测场景中表现出更强鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present NightFeats, a structured multi-agent retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system submitted to the MMU-RAGent competition at NeurIPS 2025, where it was awarded Best Dynamic Evaluation in the text-to-text track. Rather than targeting benchmark maximization, this work proposes a principled pipeline that decomposes knowledge synthesis into three coordinated phases: retrieval, curation, and composition, each governed by explicit intermediate representations and handoff contracts. Inspired by Agentic Context Engineering (ACE), the system introduces temporal-semantic reranking, bounded contradiction reconciliation, and citation-preserving composition as core architectural primitives. Competition results show that NightFeats surpasses proprietary baselines including Claude-SonnetV2 and Nova-Pro on LLM-as-a-Judge and Human Likert evaluations, confirming that architectural transparency and verifiable evidence grounding are better aligned with human preferences than systems optimizing narrowly for automatic similarity metrics.

</details>


### [55] [Detecting AI-Generated Content on Social Media with Multi-modal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11200)
*Chenyang Yang,Shen Yan,Yibo Yang,Litao Hu,Yuchen Liu,Yuan Zeng,Hanchao Yu,Yinan Zhu,Sumedha Singla,Brian Vanover,Huijun Qian,Zihao Wang,Fujun Liu,Aashu Singh,Jianyu Wang,Xuewen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向社交媒体场景的多模态AIGC检测与解释流水线，通过持续构建多样化数据并训练紧凑型视觉-语言模型，实现更强泛化、可解释检测，并在真实平台部署中带来正向业务效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC检测方法在面对新生成模型时泛化能力不足，常依赖单一模态，且缺少可解释性；而社交媒体中逼真生成图像和视频的传播带来垃圾内容、误导、操纵和欺诈等风险，因此需要适用于动态真实环境的检测方案。

Method: 作者持续收集和整理多平台、多模态社交媒体数据，并训练一个紧凑型视觉-语言模型，用于同时完成AIGC检测和解释生成；该模型被集成到社交媒体平台的帖子推荐流程中进行实际部署验证。

Result: 模型在公开基准上达到最先进检测性能，并在内部跨平台社交媒体数据集上展现出鲁棒的检测与解释能力；实际部署后，对用户参与度等下游指标产生了积极影响。

Conclusion: 通过多模态数据持续迭代和紧凑视觉-语言模型，可以在动态社交媒体环境中实现有效、可解释且可部署的AIGC检测。

Abstract: Generative AI has enabled the creation of photorealistic images and videos that are increasingly disseminated on social media, often used for spam, misinformation, manipulation, and fraud. Existing AI-generated content (AIGC) detection methods face challenges including poor generalization to new generation models, reliance on single modalities, and lack of interpretable explanations. We present our pipeline that mitigates these issues by continuously curating diverse multi-modal social media data and training a compact vision-language model for detection and explanation. Our model achieves state-of-the-art detection performance on public benchmarks and demonstrates robust detection and explanation capabilities on internal social media datasets across multiple platforms. We deployed our model for post recommendation on social media platforms and observed positive downstream impacts on user engagement, demonstrating that it is feasible to perform effective AIGC detection in dynamic, real-world social media environments.

</details>


### [56] [One Jailbreak, Many Tongues: Learning Language-Insensitive Intention Representations for Multilingual Jailbreak Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11202)
*Shuyu Jiang,Kaiyu Xu,Xingshu Chen,Hao Ren,Rui Tang,Yi Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Hongwei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MLJailDe，一个面向多语言场景的越狱检测框架，通过多语言回译增强、跨语言表征约束与类别不平衡感知分类目标，提升多语言鲁棒性和跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的多语言能力快速提升，但安全训练和越狱防御主要集中在主导语言；多语言监督数据稀缺以及语言差异导致的表征分散，使得非主导语言场景存在可被利用的安全检测缺口。

Method: 方法包括：构建覆盖 11 种语言的多语言回译数据增强算法，生成语义一致且功能有效的数据集；使用相对距离约束减少跨语言表征分散，使相同意图的越狱提示在不同语言中形成一致聚类；引入类别不平衡感知分类目标，以学习更可靠的多语言决策边界。

Result: 实验显示 MLJailDe 在多种语言上优于现有先进基线，取得 98.5% 的 F1 分数；在未见语言上平均 F1 达到 97.1%，表现出较强的跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLJailDe 有效缓解了多语言越狱检测中的数据稀缺、表征分散和类别不平衡问题，为全球多语言 LLM 安全防护提供了更稳健的检测方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in applications for global multilingual users, yet safety training remains concentrated in dominant languages and has not progressed in parallel with multilingual capability, creating exploitable gaps for jailbreak attacks. Current jailbreak defenses are largely developed and evaluated in dominant languages, and their effectiveness is limited by the scarcity of aligned multilingual supervision and representations dispersion caused by language variation. To address this issue, we propose MLJailDe, a multilingual jailbreak detection framework designed to improve both multilingual robustness and cross-lingual generalization. MLJailDe first introduces a multilingual back-translation data augmentation algorithm to construct a semantically consistent and functionally effective dataset spanning 11 languages, consisting of 2,232 benign and 1,239 jailbreak samples. On this basis, MLJailDe employs relative-distance constraints to reduce cross-lingual representation dispersion and encourage jailbreak prompts with similar intent to form consistent clusters across languages, while an imbalance-aware classification objective is further used to alleviate class imbalance and learn more reliable multilingual decision boundaries. Experimental results show that MLJailDe outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple languages, achieving an F1 score of 98.5\%, and obtains an average F1 score of 97.1\% on unseen languages, demonstrating strong effectiveness and cross-lingual generalization.

</details>


### [57] [LatticeBridge: Rare-Event Sequential Inference for Faithful Structured Sequence Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11203)
*Faruk Alpay,Bugra Kilictas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将带多重输入约束的结构化序列生成视为稀有事件序贯推断问题，提出 LatticeBridge 解码框架，通过自动机构建与扭曲 SMC 粒子解码提升约束锚点满足率与覆盖率，并系统评估忠实性、重叠度和延迟之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准解码方法容易生成流畅但未同时满足所有输入派生约束的文本；在需要多个锚点共同出现的任务中，满足所有约束的续写概率质量较低，因此需要一种更适合稀有约束满足场景的解码方法。

Method: LatticeBridge 结合紧凑前缀语言模型、按实例编译的表层自动机，以及带重采样、多级分裂和源支持 proposal 项的扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）解码器。约束表示由每个输入实例自动编译，不依赖人工词汇类别。

Result: 在 CommonGen、E2E NLG 和 WikiBio 的 2,610 个可达验证任务上，该粒子解码器在共享 proposal 模型下，相比 greedy、beam-filtered 和 best-of-k ancestral 基线提升了精确锚点满足率与平均锚点覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，把约束生成建模为稀有事件推断并采用 SMC 粒子解码，可更有效地实现输入约束；同时仅看精确锚点满足不足以保证忠实性，因此需要联合报告源覆盖、源侵入、重叠、运行时与粒子统计等指标来刻画质量—延迟前沿。

Abstract: Structured sequence generation often requires a model to satisfy several input-derived constraints in a single output. Standard decoding methods may assign high probability to fluent continuations while placing low mass on continuations that realize all required anchors jointly. We study this regime as a rare-event sequential inference problem. LatticeBridge combines a compact prefix language model, instance-compiled surface automata, and a twisted sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) decoder with resampling, multilevel splitting, and a source-support proposal term derived from instance-provided phrases. The constraint representation is compiled from each input instance and does not rely on manually curated lexical classes. On 2,610 attainable validation tasks spanning CommonGen, E2E NLG, and WikiBio, the particle decoder improves exact anchor satisfaction and mean anchor coverage over greedy, beam-filtered, and best-of-k ancestral baselines under a shared proposal model. Since exact anchor satisfaction alone does not rule out unsupported attribute substitutions, the evaluation reports required-anchor coverage, source coverage, source-intrusion diagnostics, overlap, runtime, and particle statistics jointly. The benchmark characterizes the faithfulness-overlap-latency frontier under a fixed proposal model.

</details>


### [58] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Safety Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11204)
*Jonas Grill,Thomas Bayer,Sören Berlinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评测多种大语言模型在安全数据表（SDS）结构化信息抽取任务中的表现，发现文本管线优于多模态管线，但当前通用模型准确率仍不足以支持无人监督的工业部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDS文档格式异构、字段复杂，传统规则方法泛化能力有限；工业安全场景又要求高可靠性，因此需要系统评估先进LLM在自动化SDS数据抽取中的可行性、成本与效率。

Method: 研究比较Gemini 1.5 Pro、GPT-4o、Claude 3.7 Sonnet和Llama 3.1-70B四类模型，并在zero-shot、few-shot和chain-of-thought三种提示策略下，分别测试文本式与多模态处理管线；评估指标包括准确率、延迟和成本，覆盖超过50,000个抽取字段。

Result: 文本抽取管线在所有指标上均稳定优于多模态处理。Gemini 1.5 Pro结合Chain-of-Thought提示达到最高准确率84%，高于GPT-4o的81%和Claude 3.7 Sonnet的79%；但所有模型均未达到实际可靠部署通常要求的90%准确率阈值。

Conclusion: 通用LLM目前尚不足以在安全关键的工业SDS抽取任务中实现无人监督部署，但具备较大潜力。后续应关注领域适配微调、模型校准以及引入Human-in-the-Loop验证机制，以提升安全关键场景下的可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate extraction of structured information from Safety Data Sheets (SDS) remains challenging in industrial safety due to heterogeneous document formats and the limitations of traditional rule-based methods. This study benchmarks state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) for automated SDS data extraction, comparing text-based and multimodal processing pipelines. We systematically evaluate four models: Gemini 1.5 Pro, GPT-4o, Claude 3.7 Sonnet, and Llama 3.1-70B, across three prompting strategies: zero-shot, few-shot, and chain-of-thought. The evaluation framework assessed accuracy, latency, and cost across more than 50,000 extracted data fields. Results show that text-based extraction consistently outperforms multimodal processing across all metrics. Gemini 1.5 Pro combined with a Chain-of-Thought prompt achieved the highest accuracy (84%), outperforming GPT-4o (81%) and Claude 3.7 Sonnet (79%). However, no model surpassed the 90% accuracy threshold commonly required for reliable real-world deployment. These findings indicate that general-purpose LLMs are not yet robust enough for unsupervised industrial use, though performance suggests strong potential with task-specific fine-tuning. Future research should focus on domain-adapted training, model calibration, and the integration of Human-in-the-Loop verification to ensure safety-critical reliability.

</details>


### [59] [Compatibility-Aware Dynamic Fine-Tuning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11206)
*Yucheng Zhou,Junwei Sheng,Qianning Wang,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CADFT，在DFT的token级动态稳定基础上加入样本级兼容性控制，以降低大规模指令数据异质性导致的高方差更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT虽是LLM对齐的主流方法，但存在优化不稳定与泛化受限问题；DFT缓解了token级梯度缩放异常，却默认所有示范样本同等适合作为学习目标，忽略了指令数据中示范与当前策略不匹配带来的样本级高方差。

Method: 提出Compatibility-Aware Dynamic Fine-Tuning（CADFT）：基于模型似然构造动态、依赖当前策略的兼容性信号，对监督更新进行样本级调制，抑制不兼容示范产生的高方差梯度；同时提出延迟、低频的兼容性引导重写策略，将长期不兼容示范转化为更可学习的目标。

Result: 大量实验表明，CADFT在训练稳定性、泛化能力以及冷启动强化学习初始化方面优于相关方法，同时保持完全监督训练，不依赖显式奖励模型。

Conclusion: CADFT可被理解为一种方差受控估计器，将DFT的token级稳定化推广到样本级，为异质指令数据下的稳定SFT提供了更原则化的解决方案。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) is the predominant paradigm for aligning large language models (LLMs), yet it suffers from optimization instability and limited generalization. Recent work attributes this issue to pathological gradient scaling and proposes Dynamic Fine-Tuning (DFT) to correct it at the token level. However, DFT assumes all demonstrations are equally suitable learning targets, an assumption violated by the strong heterogeneity of large-scale instruction data, where demonstration-policy mismatch induces high-variance updates at the sample level. We introduce Compatibility-Aware Dynamic Fine-Tuning (CADFT), a principled extension of DFT that controls sample-level optimization variance. CADFT derives a dynamic, policy-dependent compatibility signal from model likelihoods to modulate supervised updates, suppressing high-variance gradients from incompatible demonstrations. We further propose a delayed, low-frequency compatibility-guided rewriting strategy to transform persistently incompatible demonstrations into learnable targets. We show that CADFT can be interpreted as a variance-controlled estimator that generalizes token-level stabilization in DFT to the sample level. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved stability, generalization, and cold-start reinforcement learning initialization, while remaining fully supervised and independent of explicit reward modeling.

</details>


### [60] [BioDivergence: A Benchmark and Evaluation Framework for Hidden Contextual Contradictions in Biomedical Abstracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11208)
*Elias Hossain,Sanjeda Sara Jennifer,Sabera Akter Bushra,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 BioDivergence，用于评估生物医学研究结论之间的“情境性分歧”，而非简单判定为蕴含、矛盾或中立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学文献中的发现常因队列、地域、检测方案、疾病亚型和临床场景差异而呈现表面冲突；现有 NLI 与科学事实验证基准难以刻画这些冲突背后的上下文结构，容易把局部有效的差异误判为直接矛盾。

Method: 作者构建 BioDivergence 评估框架，包括六类冲突分类法、13 个维度的分歧本体，并要求对每对声明输出四类结构化结果：冲突类型、分歧轴、主导混杂因素和调和性解释。同时发布 BioDivergence-Silver-v1.0，一个覆盖五个生物医学领域、包含 11,865 对声明、文章不重叠的银标基准，并提供传统去重版本用于对比。

Result: 实验显示，文章不重叠版本与传统去重版本之间存在显著模型排名差异；微调参考模型在文章不重叠设置下下降约 12 个百分点。Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3 在包含 842 个样本的主测试集上达到 0.5523 accuracy 和 0.3894 contextual-F1。

Conclusion: BioDivergence 能更真实地区分情境性分歧与直接矛盾，并有助于区分模型的文章级记忆与真正的任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Biomedical findings often seem to conflict across studies, but many of these differences are context-dependent rather than true contradictions. Variations in cohort, geography, assay protocol, disease subtype, and clinical setting can make both claims locally valid. Existing NLI and scientific claim-verification benchmarks reduce such cases to entailment, contradiction, or neutral, failing to capture the contextual structure behind divergence. To address this, we introduce BioDivergence, an evaluation framework with a six-class conflict taxonomy, a 13-axis divergence ontology, and four structured outputs per claim pair: conflict type, divergence axes, dominant confounder, and reconciliation explanation. We release BioDivergence-Silver-v1.0, an article-disjoint silver benchmark of 11,865 claim pairs across five biomedical domains, alongside a legacy deduplicated variant for comparison. Results show notable ranking differences between the two variants, with the fine-tuned reference model dropping about 12 points under the article-disjoint setting, while Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3 achieves 0.5523 accuracy and 0.3894 contextual-F1 on the 842-example primary test set. BioDivergence offers a more faithful way to distinguish contextual divergence from direct contradiction and to separate article-level memorization from genuine task learning.

</details>


### [61] [ProcessThinker: Enhancing Multi-modal Large Language Models Reasoning via Rollout-based Process Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11209)
*Jingpei Wu,Xiao Han,Weixiang Shen,Boer Zhang,Zifeng Ding,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProcessThinker 是一种无需显式训练过程奖励模型的多模态推理后训练流程，通过 rollout 估计中间步骤质量，为视觉问答中的多步推理提供更密集的过程监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 RLVR/GRPO 的多模态推理训练多依赖最终答案的稀疏奖励，难以区分错误源于末端小失误还是早期推理轨迹无效；而训练 PRM 又需要大量高质量 CoT 标注和额外训练成本。

Method: 先将推理轨迹重写为带步骤标签的格式并进行冷启动监督微调；随后使用 GRPO 结合格式奖励与 rollout-based process reward。对每个中间步骤，从该步骤采样多个后续推理并用最终答案验证得到的经验成功率作为该步骤奖励。

Result: 在 Video-MMMU、MMVU、VideoMathQA 和 LongVideoBench 四个视频理解/推理基准上，ProcessThinker 相比基线 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 持续取得性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法以较低额外训练成本实现了步骤级信用分配，能鼓励更可靠、一致的推理步骤，缓解多步视觉问答中的自相矛盾和推理漂移问题。

Abstract: Visual question answering increasingly requires multi-step reasoning. Recent post-training with reinforcement learning under verifiable rewards (RLVR) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) can improve multimodal reasoning, but most approaches rely on sparse outcome-only rewards. As a result, they struggle to tell whether an incorrect answer comes from a small mistake late in the reasoning or from an unhelpful trajectory from the start. A common solution is to train a process reward model (PRM) for step-level supervision, but this typically requires large-scale high-quality chain-of-thought annotations and additional training cost. We propose ProcessThinker, a practical post-training pipeline that provides step-level process rewards without training an explicit PRM. ProcessThinker first rewrites reasoning traces into a step-tagged format for cold-start supervised fine-tuning, then applies GRPO with a standard format reward and our rollout-based process reward. Concretely, for each intermediate step, we sample multiple continuations from that step and use the empirical success rate (final-answer verification) as the step reward. This gives dense credit assignment and encourages reasoning steps that more reliably support a correct conclusion, helping reduce inconsistent or self-contradictory progress across steps -- a key issue in logical reasoning. Across four challenging video benchmarks (Video-MMMU, MMVU, VideoMathQA, and LongVideoBench), ProcessThinker consistently improves over the baseline model Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct

</details>


### [62] [T2MM: An LLM Supported Architecture For Inquiry-Based Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11210)
*John Kos,Rudra Singh,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 T2MM：一种由大语言模型支持的文本到多模态交互模型生成架构，用于在 VERA 生态建模学习软件中根据学习者自然语言请求和当前模型上下文生成可交互、可调整的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学学习中的模型构建依赖可视化与交互性；现有多模态大语言模型虽已用于教育，但往往缺乏学习场景所需的视觉交互能力，尤其难以生成可被学习者继续手动调整的动态模型。

Method: 作者在 VERA 开放探究式生态建模环境中设计 T2MM 架构，使其结合学习者当前模型上下文与自然语言建模请求，生成交互式模型而非静态图像。为验证技术可行性，作者构建了程序生成的数据集，包含自然语言学习者建模请求及其目标模型，并将 T2MM 与基于大语言模型完整代码生成的基线架构进行比较。

Result: 在所有衡量的成功指标上，T2MM 均优于基线模型生成架构，显示其在生成符合目标、可交互模型方面具有更好的技术可行性与稳定性。

Conclusion: T2MM 展示了将大语言模型有效整合进探究式学习建模工具的方式，并为构建更具交互性的多模态 LLM 教育工具提供了一种可行架构。

Abstract: Model Construction is a foundational practice in science learning that relies on visualization and interactivity. Large Language Models, increasingly augmented with multimodal capabilities, have been integrated in education contexts to support learning. However, these tools lack visual interactivity that is required by some learning contexts. We introduce Text to Multimodal Model (T2MM), a robust, dynamic LLM supported architecture that assists in model construction within the open inquiry ecology-based modeling software Virtual Experimental Research Assistant (VERA). T2MM accounts for the current context of the learner's model and creates interactive models, rather than static images, enabling the model to remain responsive to manual adjustment. To measure technical feasibility, we evaluate T2MM through a custom procedurally generated dataset of natural language learner modeling requests and target models within the VERA system. T2MM outperforms a baseline model generation architecture implemented through LLM-supported full code generation, common in the literature, across all measured success metrics. Our contribution not only outlines LLM integration into a inquiry-based learning modeling tool, but also describes a possible architecture through which more interactive multimodal LLM tools can be created.

</details>


### [63] [Calibration Drift Under Reasoning: How Chain-of-Thought Budgets Induce Overconfidence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11211)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath,Harshit R. Hiremath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究链式思维推理预算对大语言模型校准性的影响，提出“推理下的校准漂移”（CDUR），发现更长推理并不总是更可靠，可能导致错误答案的过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在安全部署中需要能准确表达不确定性；CoT 通常被用于提升准确率和可靠性，但其对置信度校准的影响尚未充分理解，尤其是推理变长后是否仍能改善可靠性。

Method: 作者定义推理预算 B，分析期望校准误差 ECE(B) 可能呈现先降后升的非单调模式；提出基于自回归生成的 Hypothesis Lock-In 模型解释长推理如何形成内部一致但错误的解释；并在 Llama-3.1-8B 与 Llama-3.3-70B 上，对 47 个 reasoning-trap 问题、4 种推理预算、3 个随机种子进行经验评估；此外提出 CABStop 校准感知停止规则。

Result: Llama-3.1-8B 显示出非单调校准行为：适度推理降低校准误差，但超过任务特定阈值后校准恶化并出现过度自信。Llama-3.3-70B 的结果仅限于基线评估，无法对预算依赖效应得出确定结论。

Conclusion: 增加推理深度并不必然提升模型可靠性；在某些任务中，过长 CoT 会使模型锁定错误假设并产生高置信错误答案。部署时应监控推理预算与校准性，并可使用类似 CABStop 的停止策略缓解风险。

Abstract: The ability of large language models (LLMs) to express calibrated uncertainty is important for safe deployment. Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is widely used to improve accuracy and reliability, but its effect on calibration is not fully understood. We show that this picture is incomplete: in some settings, increasing the reasoning budget beyond a task-specific threshold can cause models to become systematically overconfident, assigning high confidence to incorrect answers. We call this phenomenon Calibration Drift Under Reasoning (CDUR) and study it both theoretically and empirically.
  We define reasoning budget B and analyze conditions under which Expected Calibration Error ECE(B) follows a non-monotonic pattern: it first decreases as reasoning corrects errors, then increases as longer reasoning produces internally consistent but incorrect explanations. We propose a Hypothesis Lock-In model based on autoregressive generation to explain this behavior.
  We evaluate Llama-3.1-8B and Llama-3.3-70B on 47 reasoning-trap questions across four reasoning budgets and three seeds (1,368 API calls; 574 valid responses). The 8B model shows non-monotonic calibration behavior, while results for the 70B model are limited to baseline evaluation and are inconclusive for budget-dependent effects.
  We introduce CABStop, a calibration-aware stopping rule that halts reasoning when confidence diverges from an auxiliary accuracy estimate. These results suggest that increasing reasoning depth does not always improve reliability and should be monitored carefully.

</details>


### [64] [A Geometric Profile of Semantic Information in Text: Frame-Conditional Uniqueness and a Trade-Off Triangle for Scalar Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11222)
*Dmitriy Kompaneets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于句子嵌入几何结构的语义内容度量框架，用三维语义画像刻画文本含义，并证明单一标量摘要存在不可同时满足多种理想性质的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息度量如 Shannon 熵只衡量符号不确定性而不关心语义；BERTScore 等成对指标只能比较两段文本，不能刻画单个文本承载的语义内容。因此需要一种能够对单篇文本语义内容进行内在度量的理论与实证框架。

Method: 作者在固定嵌入与基线条件下提出六条自然公理并证明标量度量的条件唯一性；随后构造由新颖性、广度、整合性组成的三坐标语义画像，并定义由聚类阈值 τ 决定分辨率的语义量子；进一步证明不存在一个标量能同时满足释义与拼接下的解析稳定性、跨文本尺度的序鲁棒性和跨表示可比性，并提出 S_minmax 与 S_rank 两个实用标量。

Result: 在 23 类合成文本、5 部 Project Gutenberg 小说和 3 种嵌入模型上验证了理论权衡。推荐的秩归一化配置在 28 项序关系检查中点估计通过 25 项，经 Benjamini-Hochberg 校正后通过 21 项，优于包括 unigram entropy 和基于 BERTScore 的新颖性信号在内的 7 个基线。广度坐标还与 DPP 的 log-determinant 高度相关，507 个 Gutenberg 章节上的 Spearman ρ=0.985。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，文本语义内容更适合以多维画像而非单一标量刻画；若必须使用标量，则必须在稳定性、鲁棒性和可比性之间取舍。框架为语义内容测量提供了公理化、几何化和优化论基础。

Abstract: How much meaning does a text carry? Shannon's theory measures uncertainty over symbols and is intentionally indifferent to meaning, while pairwise metrics such as BERTScore compare two texts rather than characterizing one. We develop a geometric framework that measures semantic content from the structure of a text's sentence embeddings.
  The framework has three parts. First, within a fixed embedding and baseline, six natural axioms uniquely determine a scalar measure up to scale, a frame-conditional uniqueness theorem. The resulting scalar is empirically too coarse, motivating a richer representation. Second, we propose a three-coordinate semantic profile capturing novelty (displacement from generic discourse), breadth (diversity of distinct ideas), and integration (connectedness among them), together with a discrete minimal unit (the semantic quantum) whose resolution is fixed by a clustering threshold $τ$. Third, we prove a no-go theorem: no scalar summary of the profile can simultaneously satisfy analytic stability under paraphrase and concatenation, ordinal robustness across text scales, and cross-representation comparability. We exhibit two practical scalars, $S_{\mathrm{minmax}}$ and $S_{\mathrm{rank}}$, each occupying a distinct corner of this trade-off triangle.
  Validation across 23 synthetic categories, 5 Project Gutenberg novels, and 3 embedding models confirms the trade-off. The recommended rank-normalized configuration passes 25 of 28 ordinal checks as point estimates (21 of 28 after Benjamini-Hochberg correction), outperforming seven baselines including unigram entropy and a BERTScore-based novelty signal. A separate variational result connects the breadth coordinate to the log-determinant of a determinantal point process (Spearman $ρ= 0.985$ over 507 Gutenberg chapters), giving an optimization-theoretic foundation for breadth.

</details>


### [65] [EverydayGPT: Confidence-Gated Routing for Efficient and Safe Hybrid GPT-RAG Conversational QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11212)
*Jaspreet Singh Nahal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 EverydayGPT，通过置信度门控路由在快速抽取式 RAG 与较慢 GPT 生成路径之间选择，以在基本保持问答质量的同时显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RAG 流水线对所有查询无条件执行检索与生成，导致不必要计算开销，并可能把低质量上下文传递给生成器，影响效率与可靠性。

Method: 作者构建了一个轻量级会话问答系统 EverydayGPT，核心是 Confidence-Gated Routing（CGR）机制，将路由决策形式化为基于检索距离与抽取充分性的联合策略。系统包含一个从头在 FineWeb-Edu 10B tokens 上训练的 205M 参数 GPT 骨干，并在快速 RAG 抽取路径与高成本 GPT 路径之间动态切换。

Result: CGR 使 85% 的查询无需调用约 5.9 秒的 GPT 路径，而由约 45 ms 的快速 RAG 抽取解决，多数查询延迟降低超过 120 倍；整体平均延迟降低 6.3 倍。在 500 题领域内基准上，F1 为 0.226±0.004，高于 GPT-only 的 0.171 和无条件 RAG 的 0.210。结构化 grounding 审计在样本中未发现无支撑声明。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在资源受限场景下，基于置信度的路由策略可以用较小的质量增益换取显著的效率提升。论文定位为路由策略研究，而非追求最先进性能。

Abstract: Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines route every query through retrieval and generation unconditionally, incurring unnecessary computation and propagating low-quality context to the generator. We introduce EverydayGPT, a lightweight conversational QA system built around a Confidence-Gated Routing (CGR) mechanism that formalises the routing decision as a joint policy over retrieval distance and extraction adequacy. The backbone is a 205M-parameter GPT trained from scratch on 10B tokens of FineWeb-Edu. CGR avoids invoking the costly GPT pathway (~5.9s) for 85 percent of queries by resolving them via fast RAG extraction (~45 ms), yielding over 120x latency reduction on the majority of queries while maintaining answer quality. On a 500-question in-domain benchmark, the system achieves F1 = 0.226 +/- 0.004 compared to 0.171 for GPT-only and 0.210 for unconditional RAG. Gains over strong baselines are modest but consistent, while efficiency improvements are substantial (6.3x mean latency reduction). A structured grounding audit finds no unsupported claims in the sampled set, with explicit scope limitations. We position this work as a study of routing strategies under resource constraints rather than a claim of state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [66] [Measuring language complexity from hierarchical reuse of recurring patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11531)
*Junyi Zhou,Rui Liu,Pengyu Liu,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ladderpath index，用于衡量语言复杂度；在 21 个平行语料上发现该指标跨语言近似不变，并支持等复杂度假说与复杂度权衡假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言复杂度度量常依赖具体表示或语言学标注，难以从更一般、可计算且相对表示无关的角度检验不同语言是否具有相近总复杂度。作者希望基于算法信息论提出一种可精确计算的压缩性指标，以分析语言复杂度在不同层级间的分布。

Method: 作者定义 ladderpath index：通过层级化复用重复子结构来重构序列所需的最少步骤数，作为受约束的算法可压缩性度量。随后将该方法应用于 Parallel Universal Dependencies 中 21 种语言的平行语料，并比较原始表示与统一二进制表示下的复杂度表现，同时分析字符集大小、语料长度、词汇层级与语料层级重构复杂度之间的关系。

Result: ladderpath index 在不同语言间近似保持不变，其变化幅度显著小于语料长度变化；在统一二进制表示下这种不变性更明显。研究还发现字符表大小与语料长度之间、词汇层级复杂度与语料层级复杂度之间存在权衡关系。自动识别出的可复用子结构与自然语言中的词和形态成分有重叠。

Conclusion: ladderpath index 为检验语言等复杂度和复杂度权衡提供了一种新的、可计算且较少依赖语言学预设的工具。结果表明，不同语言可能在总体复杂度上相近，但复杂度会在字符、词汇、形态和语料结构等层级间重新分配；这种现象可与认知科学中的组块化机制相联系。

Abstract: We introduce the ladderpath index as a measure of language complexity grounded in algorithmic information theory. It counts the minimum steps needed to reconstruct a sequence through hierarchical reuse of repeated substructures, capturing an exactly computable but constrained form of algorithmic compressibility related to, but distinct from, Kolmogorov complexity. We apply the ladderpath approach to 21 parallel corpora from the Parallel Universal Dependencies dataset. The ladderpath index is approximately invariant across the languages, and varies much less than the corpus length. This is more pronounced when all corpora are mapped to a unified binary representation, providing evidence for the equi-complexity hypothesis from a representation-independent perspective. We also observe trade-offs between character inventory size and corpus length, and between vocabulary-level and corpus-level reconstruction complexity, supporting the trade-off hypothesis that total complexity is conserved and redistributed across linguistic levels. The reusable substructures identified by the ladderpath approach, without any linguistic input, overlap with words and morphological components attested in the natural vocabulary. The hierarchical reuse captured by the ladderpath approach parallels the chunking mechanisms proposed in cognitive science, where the human cognitive system compresses linguistic input into nested, reusable units under shared memory and processing constraints. This connection between cognitive chunking and the ladderpath approach provides a new interpretation for the equi-complexity and trade-off hypotheses, grounding both in the shared cognitive architecture that underlies language processing across human languages.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond Compaction: Structured Context Eviction for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11213)
*Andrew Semenov,Svyatoslav Dorofeev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Context Window Lifecycle（CWL），通过语义感知的分级驱逐机制管理长程 LLM 代理的上下文，使其在固定上下文预算下维持近似无界的工作跨度，并在长任务序列中保持性能稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 LLM 代理在持续会话中会不断积累历史，导致上下文超预算或超长提示带来的性能下降。现有摘要压缩方法存在信息不可预测丢失、因果结构破坏、额外模型调用成本和压缩幻觉等问题；简单按时间截断又缺乏语义感知，可能删除仍然关键的信息。

Method: CWL 要求代理在执行过程中将轨迹标注为带类型、带依赖链接的 episode，构建 episode graph。当 token 预算超出时，采用确定性的、无需 LLM 的驱逐策略，依据 episode 类型、依赖关系、可恢复性与优先级删除内容；同时保留用户轮次和当前推理所需的探索上下文，并清理那些效果已持久化到环境中的动作 episode。

Result: 作者在长程 agentic benchmarks 上评估 CWL，展示单个代理会话可连续完成 89 个顺序任务，累计处理 8000 万 token，并且相较于每个任务独立会话，在任务准确率上没有可测量的下降。

Conclusion: CWL 通过结构化轨迹标注和确定性语义驱逐，在不依赖摘要压缩的情况下稳定控制上下文长度，保留关键因果与推理信息，为长程 LLM 代理提供了一种低成本、可解释且性能稳定的上下文生命周期管理方案。

Abstract: We present Context Window Lifecycle (CWL), a context-management scheme that gives long-horizon LLM agents an effectively unbounded working horizon. As a session accumulates history, CWL keeps the context within budget through graduated, semantically-aware eviction: the agent annotates its trajectory as typed, dependency-linked episodes as work proceeds, and a deterministic, LLM-free policy evicts content in priority order within that structure when a token budget is exceeded. CWL preserves user turns and the exploratory context the agent is actively reasoning over, while aggressively shedding action episodes whose effects are already persisted in the environment, keeping active context near a stable ceiling that also avoids the performance degradation associated with very large prompts.
  Compared to summarization-based compaction, CWL avoids four well-known limitations: unpredictable lossiness, destruction of causal structure, blocking model cost, and compression-induced hallucination. Compared to recency truncation, CWL is semantically aware: it drops the oldest-and-most-recoverable content according to the dependency graph rather than oldest-in-time regardless of relevance. We describe the annotation protocol, the episode graph, the eviction policy, and the token-accounting loop, and evaluate CWL on long-horizon agentic benchmarks: a single agent session completing 89 sequential tasks across 80 million tokens with no measurable degradation in task accuracy relative to per-task isolated sessions

</details>


### [68] [Afrispeech Semantics: Evaluating Audio Semantic Reasoning in Spoken Language Models Across Domains and Accents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11219)
*Chibuzor Okocha,Christan Grant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估音频语言模型在转写、检索、字幕和问答之外的语义与副语言推理能力，强调现有评测不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频语言模型评测主要关注转写或表层理解，缺乏对基于语音证据进行深层语义推理的系统基准；同时，口音变化、领域迁移和语义过度推断对模型表现的影响尚未被充分理解。

Method: 作者设计或采用五类语义与副语言推理任务：蕴含、一致性、合理性、口音漂移和口音约束，用于检验模型是否能以音频为主要证据判断文本假设是否可推出、矛盾或无法确定，并评估其在口音变化下的稳定性与约束性。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值或模型排名，但指出当前音频推理评测存在关键局限，现有模型在复杂语义推理、跨口音稳定性和避免过度推断方面仍有不足。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，需要超越传统语音转写和问答指标，构建更面向语义推理、口音鲁棒性和公平性的音频语言模型评测框架，以指导更稳健的ALM设计与评估。

Abstract: Audio language models (ALMs) are increasingly used for speech-based understanding, yet their ability to perform semantic reasoning beyond transcription, Text-to-Audio Retrieval, Captioning, and Question-Answering accuracy remains insufficiently benchmarked. In particular, the effects of accent variation, domain shift, and semantic over-inference on audio reasoning are poorly understood. We evaluate audio language models across five semantic and paralinguistic reasoning tasks: entailment, consistency, plausibility, accent drift, and accent restraint. Collectively, these tasks assess a model's ability to reason over spoken audio as the primary evidence source, including whether a textual hypothesis can be inferred, contradicted, or left undetermined by the audio, whether statements align or conflict with spoken content, whether claims are plausible given the discourse, and whether model predictions remain stable or appropriately constrained across accent variation. These findings highlight critical limitations in current audio reasoning evaluations and hope to provide guidance for more robust and equitable ALM design and assessment

</details>


### [69] [LifeSentence: Language models can encode human life course trajectories from longitudinal panel data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11220)
*Samuel Liu,Muchen Xi,William Yeoh,Joshua J. Jackson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 LifeSentence：将生命历程事件表示为结构化自然语言记录，并对预训练大语言模型进行指令微调，用较少纵向面板数据实现更强的人生结果预测、鲁棒性与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测个体生命结果有助于理解长寿与健康生活的形成机制。传统统计方法往往忽略生命历程的序列结构，准确率有限；而现代 Transformer 方法通常需要大规模训练数据，许多纵向面板研究难以满足。

Method: 作者将每个生命事件编码为结构化自然语言记录，基于一个 240 亿参数预训练语言模型进行指令微调，并构建覆盖预测、鲁棒性和推理的 18 项任务评估体系。模型使用约 65,000 名德国社会经济面板个体数据训练，借助预训练阶段获得的分布式知识补充有限面板数据。

Result: LifeSentence 在所有任务族上优于传统统计和深度学习基线；在联合事件与时间预测中相较最佳基线提升约三倍；在去除时间戳的事件集合中重建时间顺序时达到 91.2% Kendall’s tau。模型还能在无显式监督下从离散事件序列中恢复已知社会分层模式，如教育收益、性别工资差距和母职惩罚。

Conclusion: LifeSentence 表明，将预训练大语言模型与纵向面板数据结合，可在小于既有 Transformer 方法所需数据规模的条件下提升生命历程预测与推理能力。其自然语言接口还支持新的研究查询与反事实探索，使其兼具预测工具和人类传记机制探针的功能。

Abstract: Forecasting human life outcomes is important to gain insights into how individuals attain long and healthy lives. Conventional statistical approaches yield limited accuracy, potentially due to discarding the sequential structure of the life course. Modern methods such as transformer architectures require large scale training data that most longitudinal panel studies lack. Here we introduce LifeSentence, a model for life-course reasoning that bridges large language models with longitudinal panel data. By representing each life event as a structured natural-language record and instruction-tuning a pretrained 24-billion-parameter language model across an 18-task evaluation taxonomy spanning prediction, robustness and reasoning, LifeSentence supplements panel data with distributional knowledge already encoded during pretraining. Trained on approximately 65,000 individuals from the German Socio-Economic Panel - roughly 45 times fewer than prior transformer-based approaches - LifeSentence outperforms classical and deep learning baselines across all task families, achieving a threefold improvement in joint event-and-timing prediction from best baselines and 91.2% Kendall's tau when reconstructing chronological order from timestamp-stripped event sets. Without explicit supervision, the model recovers documented patterns of social stratification, including the education premium, the gender wage gap and the motherhood penalty, from discrete event sequences alone. A natural-language interface further enables qualitatively new research queries, such as connecting an early-life history to a specified late-life endpoint, establishing LifeSentence as both a predictive tool and a probe for counterfactual exploration of human biographies.

</details>


### [70] [Every Act Has Its Price: Compressed Moral Composition in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11232)
*Weijia Zhang,Ruiqi Chen,Yunze Xiao,Weihao Xuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Moral Trolley Arena，用于评估大语言模型如何组合多个道德证据，而不仅是偏好单一道德行为。实验发现，复合道德判断主要由组成行为强度预测，但呈压缩式、非简单加和的组合规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 道德评测多关注模型在孤立道德行为、价值或道德基础之间的偏好，但真实判断通常需要在同一选项中整合多个道德信号，因此需要评估模型的道德证据组合机制。

Method: 作者构建两阶段盲 ELO 基准：第一阶段在涵盖五类道德基础的 229 个场景语料上校准单一场景中的个体道德行为强度；第二阶段将已校准行为组合为双行为道德项目，并在受控强度网格上测量复合偏好。实验覆盖十个前沿模型。

Result: 复合判断在很大程度上可由组成行为的强度预测，但这种关系表现为一致的压缩效应，而非简单线性相加。模型还表现出非加性的强度锚定、在控制组成行为后有限的道德基础特异性残差，以及跨模型提供商高度一致的复合偏好曲面。

Conclusion: LLM 道德审计不应只评估模型对孤立道德行为的排序，还应测量其组合道德证据的规则与结构，以更贴近真实复杂判断。

Abstract: Existing LLM moral benchmarks usually ask which isolated moral act, value, or foundation a model prefers. This is useful but incomplete. Realistic judgments often require a model to combine several moral signals within the same option. We introduce **Moral Trolley Arena**, a two-stage blind ELO benchmark for measuring how LLMs compose moral evidence. The single-scene arena first calibrates individual moral acts from a 229-scenario corpus across five Moral Foundations Theory foundations; the composite arena then combines calibrated acts into two-act moral items over a controlled intensity grid and measures the resulting composite preferences. Across ten frontier models, composite judgments are largely predicted by component act strength, but the relation is consistently compressed rather than simply additive. Models also show non-additive intensity anchoring, bounded foundation-specific residuals after component control, and highly convergent composite preference surfaces across providers. These results suggest that moral audits should measure composition rules for moral evidence, not only rankings over isolated acts.

</details>


### [71] [Energy-Efficient On-Device RAG on a Mobile NPU: System Design and Benchmark on Snapdragon X Elite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11257)
*Zhiyuan Cheng,Longying Lai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个完整运行在 Snapdragon X Elite 的 Qualcomm Hexagon NPU 上的端侧 RAG 流水线，覆盖 embedding、reranking 与 LLM generation，相比 CPU/GPU 在速度与能耗上显著更优且质量无明显下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 流水线计算开销大；端侧部署有利于隐私、低延迟和离线使用，但 CPU 推理能耗高，阻碍实际应用。因此需要探索能效更高的端侧神经计算硬件来支撑完整 RAG。

Method: 作者在 Dell XPS 13 / Snapdragon X Elite 平台上，将 RAG 的 embedding、reranking、LLM generation 等神经阶段部署到 Qualcomm Hexagon NPU，并与 CPU 和 OpenCL/Adreno GPU 基线在索引与查询工作负载上进行性能、能耗和质量评测。

Result: 索引阶段 NPU 的 embedding 吞吐量较 CPU 提升 9.1 倍，系统能耗降低 12.3 倍；在 120 条 Wikipedia passage 查询基准上，NPU 的 LLM prefilling 快 18.1 倍，端到端查询延迟降低 4.0 倍，系统能耗降低 4.0 倍。GPU 比 CPU 慢 1.7 倍，且能耗为 NPU 的 6.5 倍。GPT-4.1 评估显示 NPU 质量与 CPU/GPU 基本相当。

Conclusion: 在 Snapdragon X Elite / Hexagon 类笔记本 SoC 上，NPU 能够实现实用且节能的端侧 RAG，同时不引入明显质量退化；该方向有望推广到其他成熟化的移动 NPU 平台。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines are compute-intensive, combining embedding, retrieval, reranking, and large language model (LLM) generation. Running them entirely on-device benefits privacy, latency, and offline use, but the energy cost of CPU inference is a major barrier. We present what is, to our knowledge, the first end-to-end RAG pipeline that runs all neural stages -- embedding, reranking, and LLM generation -- on the Qualcomm Hexagon NPU of the Snapdragon X Elite. Profiling on a Dell XPS 13 laptop, we compare NPU-accelerated RAG against CPU and OpenCL/Adreno GPU baselines on indexing and query workloads. On indexing, the NPU achieves 9.1x higher embedding throughput and 12.3x less system energy. On a 120-query Wikipedia-passage benchmark, it delivers 18.1x faster LLM prefilling, 4.0x lower end-to-end query latency, and 4.0x less system energy than the CPU baseline; the same workload on the integrated GPU is 1.7x slower than CPU and uses 6.5x more energy than the NPU. A GPT-4.1 LLM-as-judge evaluation finds NPU answer quality on par with CPU and GPU within evaluator noise (mean 9.32 vs. 8.95 vs. 9.03 on a 1-10 rubric), with 86.7% of queries scoring identically across all three backends. On the Snapdragon X Elite / Hexagon class of laptop SoC, the NPU thus enables practical, energy-efficient on-device RAG without quality regression -- a sustainable path toward green edge intelligence that we expect to generalize to comparable mobile NPUs (Apple Neural Engine, Intel NPU, MediaTek APU) as their software stacks mature.

</details>


### [72] [Schützen: Evaluating LLM Safety in Bulgarian and German Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11316)
*Kiril Georgiev,Yuxia Wang,Dimitar Iliyanov Dimitrov,Preslav Nakov,Ivan Koychev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Schützen：一个面向德语与保加利亚语的大语言模型安全评测数据集，用于评估模型在风险场景下的可回答性，并揭示不同语言间显著的安全行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型安全评测资源主要集中于英语和中文，难以覆盖共享社会文化、法律与伦理背景下的其他语言，尤其缺乏同时面向高资源语言与低资源语言的区域化安全评测数据。

Method: 构建德语—保加利亚语安全数据集 Schützen，覆盖德国与保加利亚相关语境下的风险性问题，并使用多语言模型与语言特定模型进行实验，比较其在不同语言中的安全回答行为。

Result: 实验发现，不同语言之间的模型安全行为存在明显差异，说明同一模型在德语和保加利亚语场景下可能表现出不同的风险处理能力。

Conclusion: 论文认为，负责任地部署大语言模型需要针对具体地区、语言和社会文化背景构建定制化安全评测资源，而不能仅依赖英语或中文中心的数据集。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed across professional domains, bringing hard-to-predict risks, including the generation of harmful or disrespectful content. Although substantial progress has been made in developing safety evaluation datasets, existing resources remain overwhelmingly English- and Chinese-centric. This limitation is particularly pronounced when evaluating languages that operate within shared sociocultural, legal, and ethical contexts. To address this gap, we introduce Schützen: a German--Bulgarian safety dataset designed to assess model answerability under risk, covering both a low-resource language (Bulgarian) and a high-resource language (German). Experiments with multilingual and language-specific LLMs reveal pronounced cross-language differences in safety behavior, highlighting the necessity of tailored, region-specific evaluation resources to support the responsible deployment of LLMs in Germany and Bulgaria. Datasets and code are available at https://github.com/xnlp-lab/Schutzen. Warning: this paper contains examples that may be offensive, harmful, or biased.

</details>


### [73] [When More Documents Hurt RAG: Mitigating Vector Search Dilution with Domain-Scoped, Model-Agnostic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11350)
*Nabaraj Subedi,Ahmed Abdelaty,Shivanand Venkanna Sheshappanavar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 MASDR-RAG，通过基于组织元数据的领域范围限定缓解大规模异构文档集合中的“向量搜索稀释”问题，并发现范围限定比复杂多智能体编排更稳定有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 RAG 扩展到大型、异构文档库，稠密相似度检索的区分能力下降，top-k 结果容易返回语义相近但上下文错误的片段；即使混合稠密+稀疏检索也会出现准确率显著下降。因此需要一种在大规模场景下提高检索精度与答案可靠性的方案。
