In TGT, each test applied to a subset of items returns a positive outcome if the number of 1's (defective items) in that subset meets or exceeds a specified threshold, and has a negative outcome otherwise. We investigate how the complexity of TGT compares to that of Classical Group Testing (CGT), corresponding to the special case of the threshold equal to one, and analyse the impact of increasing the threshold on the required number of tests.
  Our main contribution is the derivation of a sharp information-theoretic phase transition at $c_{\mathrm{inf}}^{\mathrm{TGT}}k\log(n/k)$ (non-adaptive) tests for TGT within the constant-column test design. The threshold constant $c_{\mathrm{inf}}^{\mathrm{TGT}}$ is expressed as a function of the prevalence of defectives and the threshold value. Our upper bound is derived under an analytic assumption, and we verify that this assumption is satisfied for a threshold value of 2.
  The value of $c_{\mathrm{inf}}^{\mathrm{TGT}}$ reveals that TGT on the constant-column design has the same information-theoretic behaviour as CGT in the low-prevalence regime. Yet, strikingly, at higher prevalences, the threshold leads to a significant reduction in the number of tests.
  On the other hand, we provide evidence that when the asymptotic proportion of defective items is positive, TGT actually becomes strictly harder than CGT (excluding trivial reductions).

</details>


### [108] [Color-Rule-Function Encoding for Combinatorial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11365)
*Alexander Khitun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于组合记忆的 Color-Rule-Function（CRF）编码框架，通过路径、单元取值、规则与布尔函数共同表示信息，旨在提升存储设计空间与潜在存储密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统存储方式的设计空间扩展受限，而组合记忆可通过结构化网格中的路径集合编码信息。作者希望建立一种系统化编码方法，以利用规则化和函数化表示提高可表达能力，并探索其在高密度存储中的潜在优势。

Method: CRF 方法包含四个步骤：选择网格中的路径序列；为每个单元赋值（如颜色）；根据路径上遇到的取值定义规则集合；构造决定每条路径状态的布尔函数。论文通过多个示例说明编码过程，并讨论可能的硬件实现及定制模块设计以降低硬件开销。

Result: 摘要声称 CRF 的设计空间相较传统存储具有更快的规模增长；虽然带来更高硬件复杂度，但通过定制模块可显著降低开销。估算显示，结合 CRF 编码与定制模块后，存储密度可能超过每平方厘米 Exabit 量级。

Conclusion: CRF 框架为组合记忆提供了系统编码范式，可能在数据存储密度上带来实际优势；但其可行性仍依赖硬件复杂度控制，并需进一步研究固定硬件约束下目标比特序列与 CRF 可实现序列之间的最小汉明距离问题。

Abstract: Combinatorial memory is a class of memory in which information is encoded in the set of paths through a structured mesh. In this work, we introduce a systematic encoding framework, referred to as the Color-Rule-Function (CRF) approach, for representing information in combinatorial memory. The method consists of four key steps: selecting a sequence of paths in the mesh, assigning values (e.g., colors) to each cell, defining a set of rules based on the values encountered along each path, and constructing a Boolean function that determines the state of each path. . The coding procedure is illustrated by several examples. The design space scales of the CRF scale fundamentally faster compared to conventional memory. This apparent advantage arises from the use of rule-based and functional representations but is accompanied by increased hardware complexity. A possible hardware realization of the CRF framework is discussed. Importantly, the hardware overhead can be substantially reduced through the use of customized modules. The examples of the customized design are described in the text. The combination of CRF coding with customized module design may lead to a practical advantage in data storage density. According to the estimates, the data storage density may exceed Exabit per centimeter squared. A key problem that requires further investigation is related to the minimum Hamming distance between an arbitrary target bit sequence and the closest sequence realizable within the CRF framework under fixed hardware constraints.

</details>


### [109] [Maximum Coverage Chase Decoder for Optical Interconnects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11401)
*Alessandro Cardinale,Wenqing Song,Bin Chen,Alex Alvarado,Andreas Burg,Yifei Shen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向光互连的低复杂度 Chase 译码器，通过将测试模式选择建模为广义最大覆盖问题，在保持标准 Chase 译码性能的同时显著减少测试模式数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光互连中的前向纠错译码需要在纠错性能与硬件/计算复杂度之间取得平衡。传统 Chase 译码通常需要生成并评估大量测试模式，导致复杂度和延迟较高，因此需要更高效的测试模式选择策略。

Method: 作者将 Chase 译码中的测试模式选择问题形式化为广义最大覆盖问题，从候选测试模式中选择更具代表性和覆盖能力的子集，并将该低复杂度策略应用于级联 RS-BCH 码和 oFEC 码。

Result: 在级联 RS-BCH 码上，该方法在达到标准 Chase 译码性能的同时减少了 25% 的测试模式；在 oFEC 码上减少了 61.3% 的测试模式。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在不牺牲标准 Chase 译码性能的前提下降低测试模式数量，从而降低译码复杂度，适合对吞吐率、延迟和硬件资源敏感的光互连场景。

Abstract: We propose a low-complexity Chase decoder for optical interconnects that formulates test pattern selection as a generalized maximum coverage problem. For concatenated RS-BCH and oFEC codes, our decoder achieves the standard Chase decoding performance with 25% and 61.3% fewer test patterns, respectively.

</details>


### [110] [Lifted Gabidulin Construction for LDPC Representations of Finite Geometry Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11454)
*Yifei Shen,Andreas Burg*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于铅笔选择的有限几何码稀疏化校验矩阵构造方法，用提升 Gabidulin 码显式求解常维子空间打包问题，在保持有限几何码代数优势的同时提升 BP 译码实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限几何码兼具经典分组码的代数结构与 LDPC 码的 BP 迭代译码潜力，但标准的 flats-点关联矩阵过于稠密且含大量短环，导致实际 BP 译码性能受限。

Method: 将解码矩阵稀疏化问题建模为常维子空间打包问题，并采用 lifted Gabidulin codes 显式构造 pencil selection；分别面向仿射几何与射影几何构造稀疏奇偶校验矩阵。

Result: 在码长最高 1024 的有限几何码上完成构造与验证；四个 FG 码的仿真结果显示无明显 error floor，并在块错误率 10^-7 处相比对应 5G LDPC 码约有 0.5 dB 增益。

Conclusion: 所提出的稀疏化构造有效缓解了有限几何码关联矩阵稠密和短环问题，使 FG 码能够更充分发挥代数结构与迭代译码优势，并展现出优于 5G LDPC 的高可靠性能。

Abstract: Finite geometry (FG) codes combine the algebraic properties of classical block codes with the iterative belief propagation (BP) decoding ability of low-density parity-check~(LDPC) codes. However, exploiting both advantages in practice is hindered by the fact that the standard incidence matrix between $(μ+1)$-flats and points is dense and contains many short cycles for any flat dimension $μ\geq 1$. In this work, we propose to sparsify the decoding matrix based on pencil selection, formulated as a constant-dimension subspace packing problem and solved explicitly using lifted Gabidulin codes. For both affine and projective geometries, sparse parity-check matrices are constructed and verified for FG codes of lengths up to $1024$. Simulations on four FG codes show no visible error floor and around $0.5$~dB gain over corresponding 5G LDPC codes at a block error rate of $10^{-7}$.

</details>


### [111] [Joint Movable Antenna Positioning and RIS Partitioning for Sum-Rate Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11519)
*Mohammed Saif*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出联合可移动天线（MA）与可重构智能表面（RIS）的下行通信框架，通过联合优化天线位置、子阵波束成形和RIS单元选择来提升网络和速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定天线或单独RIS设计的空间自由度有限，难以充分增强期望信号并抑制多用户干扰。作者希望利用MA的位置可调能力与RIS单元选择能力，引入额外设计自由度以提升下行多用户通信性能。

Method: 构建以最大化网络和速率为目标的联合优化问题，约束包括最小天线间距和发射功率。针对非凸难题，采用交替优化框架分解为三个子问题：使用零迫波束成形设计发射波束；通过低复杂度一维搜索进行RIS单元选择；利用块坐标下降（BCD）和基于CVX的凸优化求解MA位置优化。

Result: 仿真结果表明，相比固定MA方案以及随机配置等基准方案，所提出的联合MA-RIS框架可显著提高可达和速率。

Conclusion: 联合利用MA位置灵活性和RIS可配置性能够有效增强信号、抑制干扰，并为下行无线网络提供更高的系统吞吐量；交替优化框架为该类复杂非凸设计提供了可实现的求解路径。

Abstract: This paper investigates the utility of the movable antenna (MA) and reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) framework for downlink wireless communications. In the considered scenario, a base station (BS) is equipped with two sub-arrays of MAs transmits signals to the users via the RIS. By jointly exploiting the antenna-positioning flexibility of MAs and the RIS element selection capability, the proposed joint MA-RIS framework introduces additional design degrees of freedom to enhance desired signals and mitigate inter-user interference, thereby maximizing the network sum-rate. To this end, we formulate a joint optimization problem involving MA positioning, sub-array beamforming, and RIS element selection, subject to the minimum antenna separation and transmit power constraints. The resulting problem is highly non-convex and challenging to solve directly. To address this issue, an alternating optimization framework is developed that decomposes the problem into three tractable subproblems. Specifically, zero-forcing beamforming is employed for transmit beamformer design, a low-complexity one-dimensional search is derived for RIS element selection, and the MA positioning problem is solved using block coordinate descent (BCD) and convex optimization techniques implemented via CVX. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed joint MA-RIS framework significantly improves the achievable sum-rate compared with conventional fixed MAs and benchmark schemes with random configurations.

</details>


### [112] [Maximizing Connectivity of Uplink RIS-Assisted UAV Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11523)
*Mohammed Saif,Shahrokh Valaee*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种联合优化UAV位置、RIS辅助链路选择与RIS分区的方法，以最大化RIS辅助UAV上行网络的连通性，同时满足UE的SINR约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIS辅助UAV网络中，网络连通性受UAV位置、RIS元素分配、链路选择及UAV可靠性共同影响；直接求解联合优化问题具有高组合复杂度，因此需要高效的迭代优化框架。

Method: 以图拉普拉斯矩阵的Fiedler值表征网络连通性，构建带RIS元素分配与SINR约束的优化问题；采用扰动方法进行RIS辅助链路选择，推导RIS分区的闭式解，并在给定链路与分区后将UAV定位转化为低复杂度半定规划问题，通过CVX求解。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的UAV定位与RIS分区联合优化方案相较文献中的基准方案具有明显性能增益，能够提升网络连通性并满足SINR要求。

Conclusion: 该论文证明了联合设计RIS链路选择、RIS分区和UAV位置对提升RIS辅助UAV网络连通性是有效的，并通过低复杂度迭代方法降低了原始组合优化问题的求解难度。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a new approach for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) positioning and reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) partitioning to enhance connectivity of uplink RIS-assisted UAV networks. To achieve this, our approach optimizes RIS-aided link selection, RIS partitioning, and UAV positions to maximize network connectivity characterized by its Fiedler value. Meanwhile, it maintains a specific signal-to-interference plus noise ratio (SINR) constraint for user equipment (UE), which is influenced by RIS partitioning and UAV reliability. The network connectivity optimization problem is formulated using the Fiedler value subject to RIS elements allocation and SINR constraints. This problem is a computationally expensive combinatorial optimization, necessitating an efficient iterative approach. In particular, we propose a perturbation method for RIS-aided link selection, and derive a closed-form solution for RIS partitioning, with each partition tailored to optimize SINR for individual UAV. For the given RIS-aided links and RIS partitioning, we then show that the problem of UAV positioning can be formulated as a low complexity semi-definite programming (SDP) optimization problem, which can be solved using off-the-shelf CVX solvers. Our simulations show the potential gain of UAV positioning and RIS partitioning compared to the benchmark schemes from the literature.

</details>


### [113] [Vision-Language-Action Models Meet World Models: Embodied Agentic AI for Low-Altitude Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11618)
*Feibo Jiang,Li Dong,Lei Mao,Kezhi Wang,Cunhua Pan,Dong In Kim,Naofal Al-Dhahir*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向低空无线网络的具身智能无人机框架，以视觉-语言-动作模型为执行核心，并结合世界模型、记忆与反思机制，实现从多模态感知到连续控制的闭环自主决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无线网络由无人机等空中平台组成，可提供感知、通信与计算一体化服务。但大型生成模型在该场景中的落地仍面临动作映射能力有限、物理环境建模不足、闭环优化能力不充分等问题，限制了无人机在复杂动态环境中的自主控制能力。

Method: 研究构建了 Embodied Agentic UAV 框架：以 VLA 模型作为执行核心，建立多模态环境感知到连续控制生成的端到端决策流程；引入世界模型刻画无人机动作与环境状态演化之间的耦合关系，用于环境预测、策略验证和动态优化；同时加入记忆与反思机制，形成决策、执行、评估、更新的自适应闭环优化范式。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能够提升低空无线网络场景下无人机自主控制的鲁棒性、预测能力和可持续运行能力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将 VLA、世界模型以及记忆反思机制结合，可有效推动生成式具身智能在低空无线网络中的应用，为复杂动态空域中的自主无人机控制提供了一种系统化方案。

Abstract: Low-Altitude Wireless Networks (LAWNs), composed of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) and other aerial platforms, provide integrated perception, communication, and computation services in low-altitude airspace. However, deploying large generative models in this domain faces three major challenges: 1) Limited embodied action mapping; 2) Inadequate physical environment modeling; 3) Insufficient closed-loop optimization. To address these challenges, this study proposes an Embodied Agentic UAV framework. Centered on a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model as the execution core, the framework establishes an end-to-end embodied decision-making pipeline from multimodal environmental perception to continuous control generation. In addition, a World Model (WM) is introduced to capture the coupling between UAV actions and environmental state evolution, thereby supporting environment prediction, policy verification, and dynamic optimization. Furthermore, memory and reflection mechanisms are incorporated to form an adaptive closed-loop optimization paradigm of decision, execution, evaluation, and update, thereby enhancing the system's autonomous decision-making capability and continual evolution ability in complex dynamic environments. Experimental results validate its effectiveness in enabling robust, predictive, and sustainable autonomous control in LAWNs.

</details>


### [114] [Non-special Divisors, LCPs of Codes, and LCD Codes on Kummer Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11764)
*Huachao Zhang,Chang-An Zhao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究 Kummer 扩张上的非特殊除子刻画，并据此构造 LCP/LCD 代数几何码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性互补对（LCP）码与线性互补对偶（LCD）码在多种编码与安全相关场景中具有应用价值；其代数几何构造依赖于度为 g 或 g-1 的非特殊除子，因此需要更系统、可显式实现的非特殊除子构造方法，尤其是在 Kummer 扩张和重要曲线上。

Method: 作者考察由 y^m=f(x) 定义的 Kummer 函数域扩张，给出支撑可包含非全分歧位点的非特殊除子的算术刻画；在 GK 曲线上显式构造度 g-1 的非特殊除子；利用纯空隙构造若干具有相同重数的度 g 有效非特殊除子族；进一步建立 Kummer 扩张上 LCP 代数几何码的一般构造框架，并借助典范除子分析安全参数与 LCD AG 码构造。

Result: 论文获得了 Kummer 扩张中非特殊除子的可判定算术条件，给出了 GK 曲线上度 g-1 非特殊除子的显式实例，以及若干度 g 有效非特殊除子族；同时形成了可统一生成 LCP AG 码和 LCD AG 码的构造框架，并通过 GK 曲线与 Hermitian 曲线商的例子展示其有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将非特殊除子的理论刻画与 AG 码构造直接结合，扩展了 Kummer 扩张、GK 曲线及相关曲线商上 LCP/LCD 码的显式构造工具，为进一步设计具有可控参数的代数几何码提供了系统方法。

Abstract: Recently, constructions of linear complementary pairs (LCPs) of codes and linear complementary dual (LCD) codes on function fields have attracted considerable attention due to the wide range of applications of these codes. Such constructions rely on non-special divisors of degrees $g$ and $g-1$. In this work, we investigate Kummer extensions defined by $y^m = f(x)$ with $f(x)\in\mathbb{F}_q(x)$ and establish an arithmetic characterization of non-special divisors whose support can contain non-totally ramified places. Based on this characterization, we explicitly construct non-special divisors of degree $g-1$ on the GK curve. Moreover, utilizing pure gaps, we explicitly provide several families of effective non-special divisors of degree $g$ on Kummer extensions with the same multiplicities. We then develop a general framework for constructing LCPs of algebraic geometry (AG) codes on Kummer extensions. By virtue of canonical divisors, we show that the security parameters of LCPs of AG codes can be determined within this framework, which also enables the construction of LCD AG codes. Finally, we illustrate our results with representative examples, including LCPs of codes on the GK curve and LCD codes on quotients of the Hermitian curve.

</details>


### [115] [Segment-Wise Soft Robotics Inspired Flexible Antenna Arrays: Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11771)
*Shuaishuai Han,Konstantinos Ntougias,Elio Faddoul,Ioannis Krikidis*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出分段式软体机器人天线系统，通过软体触手各段的弯曲、伸缩与摆动实现天线空间重构，并在仿真中显著提升系统和速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可重构天线系统的空间调节能力有限，难以在紧凑阵列或复杂信道环境下实现大尺度、灵活的天线位置重构；因此需要一种结合机械形变与天线重构的新型部署方式。

Method: 作者构建分段式软体机器人天线模型，提出SEAC与HEIAC两种部署方案。SEAC在各段末端安装固定天线，通过段运动重构位置；HEIAC进一步在段内集成可重构天线，实现大尺度形变与细粒度调节结合。针对SEAC提出PDD-PGA算法求解和速率最大化问题；针对HEIAC采用BCD-PDD-PGA并结合贪婪后向天线选择，联合优化段形变、段内天线位置与激活状态。

Result: 仿真表明，两种方案均明显优于固定位置阵列和传统可重构天线基线。相较传统3D可重构阵列，SEAC和HEIAC分别获得37.9%和32.1%的和速率增益；在紧凑阵列部署中，SEAC最高可获得49.3%增益。

Conclusion: 分段式软体机器人天线通过可控机械形变扩展了天线空间重构自由度，可有效提升无线系统容量；结合可重构天线的混合方案还能提供更细粒度的部署优化。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a segment-wise soft robotic antenna (SRA) system, where each soft robotic arm referred to as a tentacle, comprises multiple independently controllable segments with bending, elongation-retraction, and sweeping motions. By adjusting segment motion parameters, the positions of surface-mounted antennas are reconfigured, distinguishing it from conventional reconfigurable antenna (RA) systems. Based on this model, we propose two antenna deployment schemes: the segmented end-antenna configuration (SEAC), where fixed antennas are mounted at the segment ends and reconfigured via segment motions; and the hybrid end-and-intermediate antenna configuration (HEIAC), where RAs are further integrated as intra-segment antennas. In HEIAC, soft-robot segment deformation provides large-scale spatial reconfiguration, while RAs enable fine-grained adjustment. For SEAC, we formulate a sum-rate maximization problem accounting for inter-segment connectivity and the nonlinear mapping from segment deformation parameters to antenna coordinates, and develop a penalty dual decomposition-projected gradient ascent (PDD-PGA) algorithm. For HEIAC, we jointly optimize segment deformation, intra-segment antenna positions, and antenna activation using a block coordinate descent (BCD)-PDD-PGA algorithm with greedy backward antenna selection. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed schemes substantially outperform fixed-position antenna arrays and conventional RA baselines. In particular, SEAC and HEIAC achieve 37.9% and 32.1% sum-rate gains over conventional 3D reconfigurable arrays, respectively, while SEAC provides up to a 49.3% gain in compact array deployments.

</details>


### [116] [STCC: A Unified Source-Channel Semantic Token Coding Framework for Semantic Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11819)
*Zhicheng Bao,Chen Dong,Sen Wang,Long Liu,Nan Ma,Hao Chen,Xiaodong Xu,Yinqiu Liu,Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 STCC，用于在噪声信道上传输基础模型所需的离散语义 token，通过学习式星座映射将信道噪声转化为语义或结构上相近的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Deep JSCC 多直接处理图像像素等原始模拟数据，难以兼容依赖离散语义 token 的基础模型；传统固定星座调制对所有 token 一视同仁，在噪声下容易产生随机且灾难性的符号错误。

Method: 提出 Semantic Token Codebook Communication（STCC）框架，其核心为 Semantic Token Codec（STC）。STC 以离散 token 为输入，采用带残差结构的 MLP 编码器学习几何结构化星座，并通过三重损失目标优化，使信道拓扑与语义嵌入空间对齐。

Result: 理论与实验表明，STCC 使噪声引起的错误更倾向于落在语义或结构相近的 token 上；在低信噪比场景下显著优于传统通信系统，且无需修改接收端。

Conclusion: STCC 为面向基础模型的离散语义 token 传输提供了统一的源信道联合编码方案，可缓解随机误码导致的性能崩溃，并将信道噪声转化为可接受的语义变化或结构失真。

Abstract: Deep Joint Source-Channel Coding (JSCC) has emerged as a promising paradigm for overcoming the ``cliff effect" in wireless communications. However, existing Deep JSCC frameworks operate directly on raw analog data such as image pixels rather than the discrete semantic tokens that foundation models require. Moreover, traditional systems employ fixed, hand-designed constellations that treat all tokens equally, leading to catastrophic random errors under channel noise. In this paper, the Semantic Token Codebook Communication (STCC) is proposed as a unified source-channel semantic token coding framework designed to transmit the discrete semantic tokens of foundation models over noisy channels. The core of STCC is the Semantic Token Codec (STC). It accepts discrete tokens as input, which maintains compatibility with foundation models while employing a residual multiple layer perceptron, i.e., MLP-based encoder that learns geometrically structured constellations optimized with a triple-loss objective. This learned mapping forces the channel topology to align with the semantic embedding space, ensuring that channel noise results in topological errors rather than random corruption. This phenomenon is theoretically and empirically characterized, identifying ``Semantic Drift" in symbolic modalities and ``Structural Distortion" in perceptual modalities, where errors shift predictions to semantically or structurally similar tokens. Extensive experiments demonstrate that STCC significantly outperforms traditional systems in low-SNR regimes, effectively converting channel noise into semantic variations without requiring receiver-side modification.

</details>


### [117] [Graphical Analysis of Lifted Product Code Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11987)
*Ragnar Freij-Hollanti,Kirsten D. Morris,Patricija Šapokaitė*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文分析 lifted product 量子低密度奇偶校验码的校验矩阵及其 Tanner 图结构，证明 H_X 与 H_Z 的 Tanner 图同构，并研究连通性与最小吸收集界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lifted product codes 是首个被证明渐近良好的 QLDPC 码族。理解其校验矩阵 H_X、H_Z 及对应 Tanner 图的结构，有助于解释译码行为，特别是误差平台现象。

Method: 作者从图论角度研究 H_X 和 H_Z 的 Tanner 图，证明二者同构；进一步分析图的组合结构，给出保证图连通性的条件，并推导最小吸收集的界。

Result: 论文证明了 H_X 与 H_Z 的 Tanner 图确实同构，建立了这些 Tanner 图连通性的充分条件，并给出了最小吸收集大小的相关界限。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了 lifted product QLDPC 码中影响译码性能的关键组合结构，为后续分析译码错误、误差平台以及码构造优化提供了图论基础。

Abstract: Lifted product codes are an important family of quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes, as they were the first QLDPC code family shown to be asymptotically good. Understanding the structure of their parity-check matrices $H_{\mathsf{X}}$ and $H_{\mathsf{Z}}$, as well as the associated Tanner graphs, is essential for analyzing their decoding behavior and error-floor performance. In this work, we show that the Tanner graphs of $H_{\mathsf{X}}$ and $H_{\mathsf{Z}}$ are indeed isomorphic, and investigate their graph-theoretical structure. We establish conditions ensuring the connectivity of these graphs and provide bounds on their minimal absorbing sets, providing new insight into the combinatorial structures influencing decoding performance.

</details>


### [118] [Reconfigurable Antennas for Next-generation Mobile Communication Networks: A Comprehensive Survey and Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12139)
*Yizhe Zhao,Long Zhang,Halvin Yang,Kun Yang,Rui Zhang,Lingyang Song,Yuanwei Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述面向6G等下一代移动通信网络的可重构天线技术，重点分析流体天线、可移动天线、夹持天线和可重构全息天线的建模、估计、性能、资源分配及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代移动通信网络需要超可靠低时延、大规模连接和智能化应用支持；传统固定天线灵活性不足，难以适应动态无线环境，因此需要能够动态调节射频特性的可重构天线。

Method: 论文采用综述与教程相结合的方法，系统梳理不同类型可重构天线的工作机制与应用潜力，并从信道建模与估计、性能分析、资源分配策略以及与新兴无线技术协同等维度展开比较分析。

Result: 文章总结了流体天线、可移动天线、夹持天线和可重构全息天线在提升链路可靠性、环境适应性、频谱/能量效率和网络智能化方面的潜力，并给出了不同技术路线的对比。

Conclusion: 可重构天线是支撑下一代移动网络的重要候选技术，但仍面临信道建模复杂、实时控制、硬件实现、资源优化和系统级集成等开放问题，未来需要在理论、算法和原型验证方面继续深入研究。

Abstract: The transition to next-generation mobile communication networks, particularly 6G, demands advanced technologies to meet the requirements for ultra-reliable, low-latency communication, massive connectivity, and intelligent applications. Reconfigurable antennas (RAs) play a crucial role in achieving these objectives by enabling dynamic adjustments to the radio frequency (RF) characteristics of antennas, such as gain, radiation pattern, impedance, and polarization. Unlike traditional fixed-position antennas, RAs can alter both their radiation patterns and positions, offering flexibility in response to varying communication environments. This paper presents a comprehensive survey and tutorial on RAs, with a focus on fluid antennas (FAs), movable antennas (MAs), pinching antennas (PAs), and reconfigurable holographic antennas (RHAs), examining their potential in next-generation mobile networks. We explore the channel modelling and estimation, performance analysis, resource allocation strategies, and their synergy with other emerging wireless technologies for each type of RA. Finally, we provide a comparative analysis of different RAs and discuss the open challenges and future research directions, offering insights and guidance for future investigations in the exciting research area.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [From Explicit Elements to Implicit Intent: A Predefined Library for Auditable Behavioral Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11207)
*Liu hung ming*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemantiClean 是一个面向电商会话数据的模块化语义信号抽取与推理框架，强调可审计性、结构治理和确定性复现，并通过 LLM 集成推理引擎支持购买意图、客户分群、产品亲和等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端预测模型通常以准确率最大化为核心，缺乏元素级透明性、可解释的决策轨迹和结构化治理能力。该工作希望在电商行为数据场景中，用可审计、可复现的语义元素体系替代黑箱式预测。

Method: 基于 OSPI 数据集，将 24 个行为元素组织为功能、交互、系统、上下文四层架构；引入 RedundancyGroup 贡献上限、TieredPenaltyCalculator 偏差惩罚和 AdaptiveConstraintMode 冷启动保护三类反膨胀机制；并实现两阶段 LLM 驱动的语义推理引擎，在推理时利用完整元素元数据。

Result: 报告中的量化结果均由 LLM-Integrated Semantic Inference Engine 生成。确定性引擎输出可实现 sigma=0 复现；LLM 相关结果在固定供应商、模型和温度设置下仍存在受控波动。当前实现中，性别推理目标不可用，并已从量化结果中排除。

Conclusion: 该框架通过牺牲少量预测性能换取元素级透明性、治理能力和可辩护的决策链，适合对可解释性和复现性要求较高的电商语义推理任务，但其 LLM 组件仍需面对输出稳定性与目标功能完整性的限制。

Abstract: We present SemantiClean, a modular framework for extracting structured semantic signals from e-commerce session data and driving pluggable inference targets including purchase intent, customer segmentation, and product affinity through a shared element library. Unlike conventional end-to-end predictors that optimise solely for accuracy, SemantiClean prioritises auditability, structural governance, and sigma=0 reproducibility, explicitly trading marginal predictive gains for element-level transparency and defensible decision trails. Built upon the Online Shoppers Purchasing Intention (OSPI) dataset, the framework organises twenty-four behavioural elements into a four-layer architecture (Functional, Interaction, Systemic, Contextual) and enforces signal quality through three anti-inflation mechanisms: RedundancyGroup contribution caps, TieredPenaltyCalculator bias penalties, and AdaptiveConstraintMode cold-start protection.This report introduces the LLM-Integrated Semantic Inference Engine, a fully implemented two-phase LLM-driven inference architecture that leverages complete element metadata at inference time. All quantitative results reported herein are produced by this engine. Deterministic engine outputs remain fully reproducible (sigma=0); LLM-dependent results (E8, E10) are subject to controlled output variability under fixed provider/model/temperature settings. The gender inference target remains non-functional in the current implementation and is excluded from all quantitative results.

</details>


### [120] [Position: Hippocampal Explicit Memory Is the Cornerstone for AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11245)
*Sangjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张：显式记忆是推动大语言模型迈向通用智能的关键基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽在多任务上表现突出，但其学习机制更类似人类隐式记忆，难以单独支撑长期战略规划、元认知、符号推理等高阶认知能力；因此需要从神经科学角度重新审视记忆机制对AGI的作用。

Method: 文章属于立场论文，主要通过类比LLM的统计学习机制与人类隐式记忆，并引入神经科学中海马体显式记忆对高阶认知功能的支持证据，进一步提出人工显式记忆系统应具备的计算需求。

Result: 论文得出观点：仅依赖隐式统计学习不足以产生AGI所需的高阶认知功能；显式记忆整合可能为长期规划、元认知和符号推理提供必要支撑。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁将显式记忆作为LLM迈向AGI的核心研究方向，并希望通过提出神经科学依据与计算需求，为未来显式记忆集成研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across various tasks, raising expectations for Artificial General Intelligence (AGI). This position paper argues that integrating explicit memory is the cornerstone for advancing LLMs toward AGI. The key reason is that the underlying learning mechanism of LLMs is highly analogous to human implicit memory. However, higher-order cognitive functions necessary for AGI, such as long-term strategic planning, metacognition, and symbolic reasoning, heavily rely on hippocampal explicit memory and cannot arise solely from implicit statistical learning. Drawing on findings from neuroscience, I advance this perspective and complement it with computational requirements for artificial explicit memory systems, hoping to foster further research and lay the groundwork for explicit memory integration.

</details>


### [121] [Can AI Agents Synthesize Scientific Conclusions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11337)
*Hayoung Jung,Pedro Viana Diniz,José Reinaldo Corrêa Roveda,Abner Fernandes da Silva,Haeun Jung,Enoch Tsai,Aleksandra Korolova,Manoel Horta Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SciConBench 与 SciConHarness，用于评估科学 AI 代理在开放域科学结论综合中的真实能力；结果显示当前前沿模型在严格无泄漏环境下事实综合质量仍然较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着科学 AI 代理被用于检索证据、跨来源推理并生成影响重要决策的结论，其在健康等高风险领域的可靠性尚不清楚；同时，现有评测可能受到数据泄漏影响，导致能力被高估。

Method: 构建包含 9.11K 个问题及专家撰写系统综述结论的大规模动态基准 SciConBench；采用专家验证的自动评估流程，将结论分解为原子事实，并用事实精确率与召回率衡量正确性和全面性；进一步提出 SciConHarness，通过受控网页交互实现 clean-room 评测，以降低数据泄漏。

Result: 评估 8 个前沿模型和深度研究代理后发现，在 clean-room 设置下最佳代理的事实 F1 仅为 0.337；clean-room 评测相比非受限评测会稳定降低性能，说明泄漏会夸大模型能力；面向消费者的代理也常生成不完整甚至矛盾的结论。

Conclusion: 可靠的科学结论综合仍是开放挑战；对开放域 AI 代理进行 clean-room 评测对于获得有效、可信的能力估计至关重要。

Abstract: Scientific AI agents increasingly retrieve evidence, reason across sources, and synthesize conclusions used in consequential decisions. Yet, their ability to do so in high-stakes domains such as health remains unclear. We introduce SciConBench, a large-scale live benchmark of 9.11K questions and expert-written conclusions from systematic reviews to evaluate open-domain scientific conclusion synthesis. The benchmark draws on an expert-validated automated evaluation pipeline that decomposes conclusions into atomic facts and measures correctness and comprehensiveness via factual precision and recall. To mitigate data leakage, we further introduce SciConHarness, a clean-room evaluation harness that equips agents with controlled web interaction to ensure valid measurement. Evaluating 8 frontier models and deep research agents, we find that factual quality remains low: under clean-room settings, the best agent achieves only a factual F1 of 0.337. Our clean-room setting consistently reduces performance relative to unconstrained evaluation, suggesting that leakage inflates estimates of models' true synthesis capabilities. Finally, we audit consumer-facing agents (e.g., Google AI Overview, OpenEvidence) and find they frequently generate incomplete and sometimes contradictory conclusions, even when the ground-truth answer is available. Overall, our results show that reliable synthesis of scientific conclusions remains an open challenge, and that clean-room evaluation is essential for assessing open-domain AI agents.

</details>


### [122] [Knowing When to Ask: Self-Gated Clarification for Hierarchical Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11349)
*Aijing Gao,Yiming Kang,Mengdie Flora Wang,Jae Oh Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ACTION-RATING，将澄清/求助作为层级推理智能体动作空间中的一类动作，与导航动作在同一序数评分尺度上竞争，从而在中间决策点显式建模何时求助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级推理的错误常发生在中间分支选择处：智能体在缺少关键信息时仍错误提交到某一分支。既有做法多把澄清视为外部不确定性触发机制，难以观察和诊断中间状态的求助行为。

Method: 作者提出 ACTION-RATING 框架，在每个决策点同时评估导航动作与询问动作，使求助成为可选择动作；并基于智能体自身评分区分两类信息寻求模式：无可行分支时的 mandatory clarification，以及存在领先候选但仍有不确定性时的 opportunistic clarification。实验在 Harmonized Tariff Schedule 分类任务上进行，覆盖 30,000 节点分类体系、三个基准、9 个大语言模型和 4 个模型家族，并定义局部诊断指标 ISE，即求助后下一步导航正确的比例。

Result: 实验观察到从 mandatory 到 opportunistic clarification 的机制转变，ISE 从 50% 提升到 74%。三个诊断对照无法复现该结构。可分离性测试显示，即使答案质量下降并导致准确率降低 18.8%，信息寻求模式（模式划分与 ISE 排名）仍保持稳定。在受控答案通道下，10 位分类准确率最高提升 16.2%。

Conclusion: ACTION-RATING 表明，求助位置选择与获得帮助的答案质量在经验上可分离；更好的求助定位可能显著释放层级推理性能潜力。但受控通道下的准确率提升应被理解为定位能力可带来的上界，而非真实部署收益估计。

Abstract: In hierarchical reasoning, failures often originate at intermediate decision points where the agent commits to a wrong branch without recognizing that it lacks critical information. Rather than treating clarification as an external uncertainty trigger, we propose ACTION-RATING, a formulation that places it inside the agent's action space on a shared ordinal scale with navigation, so that asking competes directly with acting at every decision point and help-seeking becomes observable at intermediate states. Two structurally distinct information-seeking modes emerge from the agent's own ratings: mandatory (no viable branch) and opportunistic (residual uncertainty despite a leading candidate). On Harmonized Tariff Schedule classification (30,000-node taxonomy, three benchmarks, 9~LLMs across 4 families), we observe a regime shift from mandatory to opportunistic clarification, with Information-Seeking Effectiveness (ISE), a local diagnostic defined as the fraction of help interactions followed by a correct next navigation step (not a final-task metric), rising from 50% to 74%. Three diagnostic contrasts fail to reproduce this structure. A separability test shows that the information-seeking pattern (mode split, ISE ranking) persists when answer quality is degraded (-18.8% accuracy), supporting an empirical separation between where an agent seeks help and the quality of the help it receives. Under the controlled answer channel, accuracy gains reach +16.2% at 10-digit; we read this as an upper bound on what better localization could unlock, not a deployment estimate.

</details>


### [123] [Automated Mediator for Human Negotiation: Pre-Mediation via a Structured LLM Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11379)
*Jamie Bergen,Sarit Kraus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由多个LLM模块组成的结构化自动调解系统，用于在人类谈判前的预调解阶段提供支持；实验显示其在短期自评准备效果上接近专业人类调解员，并在偏好推断任务上误差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预调解对促成互利谈判协议很重要，但现实中常因成本、时间和专业调解员可及性不足而被省略。作者希望用可扩展、低成本的自动化方式补足这一准备环节。

Method: 作者构建了一个固定顺序传递输出的LLM流水线，将预调解拆分为对话、偏好预测、回应级批判和结构化总结等模块，并区分推断、生成与评估。系统被用于整合式多议题谈判场景，并通过两项受控人类被试实验与专业人类调解员进行比较。

Result: 在短期自我报告指标上，自动调解员与人类调解员大体相当，包括对调解员的信任和达成互利协议的信心。在给定场景与提示下，其偏好推断RMSE低36%。第二项研究表明，针对性提示优化将过度肯定模式从36.6%降至16.8%，达到人类调解员基线水平。

Conclusion: 结构化LLM流水线可为谈判前预调解提供可扩展、低投入的支持，并在短期准备效果上接近人类调解员。其单方服务设计符合现有预调解实践，也便于对争议各方并行部署。

Abstract: Pre-mediation, the preparatory phase preceding direct human negotiation, plays a critical role in achieving mutually beneficial agreements, yet is often omitted due to cost, time, and limited access to trained mediators. We introduce an automated mediator for human negotiation, implemented as a structured pipeline of LLM modules, that supports pre-mediation in integrative negotiation settings. The pipeline decomposes preparation into specialized modules for dialogue, preference prediction, response-level critique, and structured summarization, separating inference, generation, and evaluation to address limitations of monolithic single-prompt approaches. We use the term "agent" for each module following common LLM-systems terminology, but the components are not autonomous and do not interact peer-to-peer; outputs are passed forward in a fixed sequence. We evaluate the system in two controlled human-subject experiments comparing AI-based pre-mediation with professional human mediators in a multi-issue negotiation scenario. On short-term self-reported measures, the automated mediator achieves preparation outcomes broadly comparable to human mediators, including trust in the mediator and confidence in reaching mutually beneficial agreements, while achieving substantially lower error on the preference-inference task under our scenario and prompts (36% lower RMSE). A second study shows that targeted prompt refinements reduce excessive affirmation patterns from 36.6% to 16.8%, matching human mediator baselines. Our findings suggest that structured LLM pipelines can provide scalable, low-effort pre-mediation support broadly comparable to human mediators on short-term self-reported preparation outcomes. The pipeline's single-party design mirrors how human mediators run pre-mediation today and enables parallel deployment across all parties to a dispute, supporting scalability.

</details>


### [124] [INFRAMIND: Infrastructure-Aware Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11440)
*Ahasan Kabir,Jiaqi Xue,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: INFRAMIND 将多智能体 LLM 编排从仅关注任务/模型特征扩展为同时感知运行时基础设施状态，通过规划、路由与调度协同优化，在保证质量的同时显著降低延迟并提升 SLO 达成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体 LLM 编排方法忽略共享 GPU 集群中的实时负载状态，导致热门模型排队严重、可替代模型空闲；在多步流水线中，单步等待会逐级累积，造成资源利用不足和端到端延迟恶化。

Method: 提出 INFRAMIND：基础设施感知规划器根据实时负载和剩余预算选择拓扑与角色；执行器在每个 agent 步骤观测模型队列深度、KV-cache 使用率和响应延迟，决定调用哪个模型及推理深度；预算感知调度器重排模型队列以优先服务紧急请求。整体被建模为分层约束 MDP，并用强化学习端到端求解。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，低负载下相比先前基线最高提升 7.6 个百分点准确率，同时延迟最高降低 7 倍；高负载下可维持最高 99.9% 的 SLO 合规率，而所有基线均低于 50%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，将动态基础设施信号纳入多智能体 LLM 的规划、执行和调度全过程，可以有效平衡质量与延迟，并在不同负载条件下显著提升系统吞吐、响应时延和服务等级目标稳定性。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent LLM orchestration methods, ranging from brute-force ensembles to learned routers, select models and topologies based on task and model features. However, these methods do not consider the runtime state of the serving infrastructure. On shared GPU clusters under concurrent load, this infrastructure blindness causes systematic resource underutilization: preferred models accumulate deep request queues while equally capable alternatives sit idle. In multi-agent pipelines, where each query triggers multiple sequential model calls, these delays then compound across every downstream step. Closing this gap is challenging because the relevant infrastructure signals (queue depths, KV-cache pressure, latencies) are dynamic and noisy, and they must drive three different decisions: planning, per-step routing, and scheduling. We introduce INFRAMIND, a framework that makes the entire multi-agent stack infrastructure-aware. An infra-aware planner conditions topology and role selection on real-time system load and remaining budget, biasing toward simpler graphs under congestion and richer ones at low load. An infra-aware executor then observes per-model queue depths, cache utilization, and response latencies at each agent step to decide which model to call and how deeply to reason; a budget-aware scheduler further reorders each model's queue so that urgent requests are served first. Cast as a hierarchical constrained MDP and solved end-to-end via reinforcement learning, the system learns to balance quality against latency automatically. Across five benchmarks, INFRAMIND delivers up to +7.6 pp accuracy over the prior baseline at low load with up to 7x lower latency, and sustains up to 99.9% SLO compliance under high load where every baseline drops below 50%.

</details>


### [125] [Forecasting Future Behavior as a Learning Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11445)
*Mosh Levy,Yoav Goldberg,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将大推理模型的行为预测直接建模为可学习任务，训练 Behavior Forecaster 从单条推理轨迹预测模型未来行为，性能优于强基线且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信任建立依赖解释模型如何工作，再据此预测其在新输入上的行为；但对大推理模型而言，单 token 解释难以扩展到长推理轨迹，且自然语言形式的推理轨迹未必忠实，因此需要绕开解释、直接预测行为的替代方案。

Method: 作者训练 Behavior Forecasters，使其以单条 reasoning trajectory 为输入，预测通常希望由解释提供的行为信息。训练数据通过无人工标注地查询目标大推理模型获得；推理阶段只需一次前向传播。实验包含两个任务：预测模型重跑时重复答案的概率，以及移除输入部分后答案如何变化。

Result: 在三个多样化推理数据集上的两个任务中，训练得到的 Behavior Forecasters 比将同一轨迹作为朴素读者读取的 GPT-5.4 和 Claude Opus-4.6 更准确，同时推理成本显著更低。实验还发现，端到端微调 backbone 以及从目标 LRM 初始化都是获得强性能的必要因素。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹中包含有关大推理模型未来行为的可学习信号，这些信号超出了朴素自然语言阅读所能提取的信息；直接训练行为预测器可能成为解释方法之外的一种低成本、有效的模型行为预测路径。

Abstract: Trust in an AI system is often anchored by explanations of how it works, which one then uses to forecast its behavior on new inputs. For large reasoning models (LRMs), this conventional route is particularly difficult to follow: explanation methods for single token generations do not naturally generalize to long trajectories, and the trajectories themselves are often not faithful when read as natural language. We propose an alternative that bypasses the explanation step: treat behavior forecasting as a learnable task and train Behavior Forecasters that operates on a single reasoning trajectory to make the same forecasts one would typically seek from an explanation. The forecaster's training data is obtained by querying the LRM with no human annotation, and its inference is done in a single forward pass. We instantiate this approach on two tasks: how likely the LRM is to repeat its answer on re-runs, and how removing parts of the input changes its answer. We evaluate this approach on both tasks across three diverse reasoning datasets and find that trained Behavior Forecasters are more accurate than GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus-4.6 reading the same trajectories as naive readers, at a small fraction of their inference cost. We find that fine-tuning the backbone end-to-end and initializing it from the target LRM are each necessary for strong performance. These results show that the reasoning trajectory carries information about the LRM's future behavior that goes beyond what naive reading conveys.

</details>


### [126] [Search Discipline for Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11522)
*Adithya Srinivasan,Devesh Paragiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出自动科研代理用单一聚合指标评估多维候选方案时，可能出现“聚合分数最优但关键子结构失效”的排序反转；作者提出外部控制循环基于可审查的分解证据进行审计和决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动科研代理通常围绕一个聚合指标提出、评估并选择候选方案，但科学有效性往往存在于区域、切片或队列等异质子结构中。若只看汇总数值，可能掩盖局部失效并导致错误选择。

Method: 作者在 Ecosystem Demography 模型的火模型任务中展示该反转现象：比较全局得分接近的候选方案，并审查其按区域分解后的行为。随后提出一种搜索纪律协议：将最终决策移出生成候选的代理，交由外部控制循环在代理决策后审计每个候选的分解效果证据。

Result: 实验显示，全局最高分候选与略低分候选在总体分数上处于噪声范围内，但最高分候选会破坏受保护的寒带区域，而略低分候选能够保持这些区域的合理行为。关键差异来自区域级行为，而非聚合分数。

Conclusion: 单一聚合指标不足以验证多维科学候选方案，可能导致错误接受。应采用外部、可审查的分解行为审计机制，对代理接受的候选进行降级或重新开启搜索，从而以候选效果证据而非单一分数作为决策依据。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents now propose, evaluate, and select scientific candidates against a metric, and that metric is usually an aggregate reduced over a heterogeneous space of regions, slices, or cohorts. We show that when scientific validity lives in that disaggregated structure, the aggregate can rank the wrong candidate first. The headline number improves while the structure underneath inverts, so a decision made on the number accepts a candidate that quietly breaks the model. The failure is not domain-specific. It appears wherever a candidate's validity is multi-dimensional but its verifier is a single reduction.
  We demonstrate the inversion on a fire-model task in the Ecosystem Demography model. The highest-scoring candidate and a slightly lower one are within noise of each other on global score, yet the top-scoring one collapses the protected boreal regions while the other preserves them. What separates them is the per-region behavior, not the headline number.
  This decision should not be left to the agent that produced the candidates. The agent optimizing the score is the last party likely to catch the score being wrong, and a prompt has no remaining turn once the agent has stopped. We move the decision to an external control loop that audits each candidate on its disaggregated behavior and acts after the agent has decided. It can demote a candidate the agent would have accepted, and it can reopen a run the agent had declared finished. Our contribution is the inversion finding itself, and a search-discipline protocol that decides on reviewable candidate-effect evidence instead of the score.

</details>


### [127] [MoCA-Agent: A Market-of-Claims Code Agent for Financial and Numerical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11537)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,AbdelRahim A. Elmadany,Sameh Al Natour,Hasan Cavusoglu,Adam Jatowt,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MOCA-Agent：一种面向金融与表格问答的“主张市场”代码代理，通过原子主张级验证而非整答案辩论来提升数值推理可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融和表格问答高度依赖精确事实、公式、单位、符号和尺度；任何单元格误读或运算错误都可能生成看似合理但错误的答案，因此需要更细粒度、可验证的推理机制。

Method: 系统将问题分解为带类型的原子主张，由专门的交易代理对主张进行买入/卖出式判断，并通过市场清算得到置信度加权的接受/拒绝结果；随后基于市场支持的证据合成可执行 Python 程序，再由代码感知验证器检查执行、结构一致性和常见金融推理错误，并最多进行一轮市场感知修复。

Result: 在十个公开基准上，使用固定 Qwen3.6-27B 骨干模型取得较强结果，包括 FinQA 78.3%、FinanceMath 76.0%、MultiHiertt 71.2%、ESGenius 86.9%，以及 FinChart-Bench 平均 85.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，相比在完整答案层面聚合意见，在原子主张层面聚合证据能增强高风险数值推理任务的鲁棒性和可信度。

Abstract: Financial and tabular question answering requires more than fluent reasoning: answers must be grounded in the exact facts, formulas, units, signs, and scales that support them. A single misread cell or incorrect operation can silently produce a plausible but wrong result. We introduce \textsc{MOCA-Agent}, a market-of-claims code agent that replaces free-form multi-agent debate with claim-level verification. The system decomposes each question into typed atomic claims, asks specialist trader agents to buy or sell those claims, clears their orders into confidence-weighted accept/reject decisions, and synthesizes an executable Python program from market-supported evidence. A code-aware verifier then checks the program for execution, structural consistency, and common financial reasoning errors, with at most one market-aware repair round. Across ten public benchmarks spanning financial numerical reasoning, general tabular reasoning, ESG question answering, and multimodal chart reasoning, \textsc{MOCA-Agent} achieves strong performance using a fixed Qwen3.6-27B backbone, including $78.3\%$ on FinQA, $76.0\%$ on FinanceMath, $71.2\%$ on MultiHiertt, $86.9\%$ on ESGenius, and $85.6\%$ average on FinChart-Bench. These results show that aggregating evidence at the level of atomic claims, rather than whole answers, improves robustness in high-stakes numerical reasoning.\footnote{The code and data are available: https://github.com/UBC-NLP/MoCA-Agent.

</details>


### [128] [SkillJuror: Measuring How Agent Skill Organization Changes Runtime Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11543)
*Zhiyu Chen,Zihan Guo,Bo Huang,Bingwei Lu,Jianghao Lin,Yuanjian Zhou,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SkillJuror 框架，用于在任务知识固定的条件下评估 LLM Agent 技能写法；结果表明 Progressive Disclosure 会显著改变代理对技能资源的检索与使用行为，并带来小幅但任务依赖的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agent Skills 基准通常无法区分技能内容本身与技能组织方式的影响。作者希望研究：在相同任务知识下，将技能写成渐进披露式结构是否比扁平化结构更有利于 LLM Agent 在推理时调用和应用程序性知识。

Method: 作者提出 SkillJuror，通过语义受控的技能变体、匹配的多轮试验和轨迹证据来比较不同 Skill 写作范式。核心对比是 Progressive Disclosure（简洁根文件按需指向支持资源）与归一化 flat baseline（扁平基线）。实验在包含 82 个任务的 SkillsBench 上进行，共进行 410 个匹配试验。

Result: Progressive Disclosure 明显改变运行时行为：每条轨迹触达的不同 Skill 资源数从 1.18 增至 3.85，有效 uptake 事件从 1.33 增至 3.92；在 410 个匹配试验中，相比扁平基线多通过 17 次验证，提升约 +4.1%。收益具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: Skill 的组织方式并非单纯呈现形式，而会影响 Agent 如何搜索和应用程序性知识。Progressive Disclosure 在支持资源能指导实现、检查或修复时更有帮助；当任务依赖精确输出规范、数值阈值或长流程 artifact 生成时效果较弱。

Abstract: Agent Skills augment large language model (LLM) agents with procedural knowledge at inference time, but current benchmarks rarely distinguish what a Skill says from how it is organized. We study this distinction through Progressive Disclosure, where a concise root file points agents to supporting resources on demand, and compare it with a normalized flat baseline. We present SkillJuror, a framework for evaluating Skill writing paradigms through semantically controlled variants, matched multi-trial evaluations, and trajectory evidence while holding task knowledge fixed. In an 82-task SkillsBench study, Progressive Disclosure changes runtime behavior before aggregate outcomes: distinct Skill resources touched per trajectory rise from 1.18 to 3.85, and effective uptake events rise from 1.33 to 3.92. It also yields 17 additional verifier-passing trials out of 410 matched trials (+4.1%) over the normalized flat baseline. The benefit is task-dependent. Progressive Disclosure helps when supporting resources guide implementation, checking, or repair, but is weaker when success hinges on exact output conventions, numerical thresholds, or long artifact-generation pipelines. These results show that Skill organization is not mere presentation: it can change how agents search and apply procedural knowledge, while outcome gains depend on whether the exposed resources are actionable for the task. Code is available at https://github.com/zhiyuchen-ai/skill-juror.

</details>


### [129] [HERO: Hindsight-Enhanced Reflection from Environment Observations for Agentic Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11559)
*Haoran Liu,Yuwei Zhang,Xiyao Li,Bohan Lyu,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HERO：一种用于多轮智能体强化学习的 hindsight-enhanced self-distillation 框架，通过利用下一步环境观测生成局部对齐的回合级诊断，改善中间步骤的信用分配与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习主要依赖轨迹终局结果优化多轮智能体，导致难以判断每个中间回合动作的贡献。近期 on-policy self-distillation 可将特权反馈转化为稠密 token 级监督，但直接扩展到多轮场景会出现性能下降。作者认为原因在于成功轨迹或终局结果等特权反馈与学生模型当前决策上下文之间缺乏对齐。

Method: 作者提出 HERO。每次 rollout 后，框架基于已完成交互进行 hindsight 反思，将每一步之后的环境观测转化为紧凑的回合级诊断反馈。该诊断围绕原动作是否必要、是否有效、失败原因等可操作信息展开，从而为对应回合提供局部对齐监督，用于自蒸馏训练。

Result: 在 TauBench 和 WebShop 上，HERO 相比仅使用环境反馈的自蒸馏方法和 GRPO，提高了任务成功率，并减少了不必要的交互轮数。尤其在训练轮数预算有限、成功 rollout 稀少、GRPO 奖励对比信号较弱时，HERO 表现更突出。

Conclusion: HERO 表明，在多轮智能体训练中，将下一步环境观测转化为与当前动作局部对齐的 hindsight 诊断，可以缓解终局奖励导致的信用分配问题，提升自蒸馏在多轮决策任务中的有效性与样本效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically improves multi-turn agent capabilities through the terminal outcome of the trajectories, which makes it difficult to determine credit assignments for each intermediate turns. Recent on-policy self-distillation methods offer a promising alternative by converting privileged feedback into dense token-level supervision through a self-teacher. Our study is motivated by the unexpected performance degradation observed when naively extending this paradigm to multi-turn settings, which we attribute to a lack of alignment between privileged feedback, such as successful trajectories or terminal outcomes, and the student's current decision context. We introduce HERO, a hindsight-enhanced self-distillation framework that uses next environment observations as locally aligned feedback. After each rollout, HERO reflects on the completed interaction to convert each observation into a compact turn-level diagnosis, that captures actionable feedback about the original action such as its necessity, validity or failure cause. On TauBench and WebShop, HERO improves task success and reduces unnecessary turns over environment-feedback-only self-distillation and GRPO. It is especially effective under limited training turn budgets, where successful rollouts are rare and GRPO provides weak reward-contrast signals.

</details>


### [130] [Belief-Space Control for Personalized Cancer Treatment via Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10376)
*Deniz Sargun,H. Bugra Tulay,C. Emre Koksal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将癌症治疗建模为部分可观测、具有潜在患者异质性且受医疗测量预算约束的序贯决策问题，并提出基于主动推断的信念空间规划框架，在真实临床数据上实现患者分类与治疗决策优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习通常假设治疗策略主要控制状态轨迹，但癌症治疗会永久改变患者的转移动态；同时临床环境存在观测不完全、患者异质性以及测量成本/预算限制，因此需要一种能同时处理治疗控制与信息获取的决策框架。

Method: 作者将癌症治疗表述为信念空间规划问题，采用主动推断方法，推导期望自由能目标函数，将目标导向控制与信息获取统一到同一优化准则中，并显式纳入医疗测量预算和治疗约束；框架在AACR Project GENIE Biopharma Collaborative真实临床癌症数据集上实现与评估。

Result: 在真实临床数据上的实验显示，该方法能够在现实测量和治疗约束下同时实现患者类别识别/分层与较高治疗效果。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，主动推断与信念空间规划适合处理癌症治疗中的部分可观测性、动态改变和测量预算限制，为个体化治疗决策提供了一个兼顾疗效与信息价值的建模方向。

Abstract: Cancer treatment is at the core a sequential decision-making problem with partial observability, latent patient heterogeneity, and explicit constraints on the budget for medical measurements. Unlike standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches that control state trajectories, cancer treatments permanently modify patients' transition dynamics, changing how states evolve over time. We model cancer treatment as a belief-space planning problem using active inference, deriving an expected free-energy objective that unifies goal-directed control and information acquisition under measurement budgets without. We implement this framework using real clinical cancer data from the AACR Project GENIE Biopharma Collaborative dataset. Results on clinical data demonstrate a simultaneous patient categorization and high treatment efficacy, under real measurement and treatment constraints.

</details>


### [131] [Architecture-Aware Reinforcement Learning Makes Sliding-Window Attention Competitive in Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11634)
*Kai Liu,Peijie Dong,Xinchen Xie,Jianfei Gao,Qipeng Guo,Xiaowen Chu,Shaoting Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究 SWARR：通过监督微调将预训练全注意力模型高效转换为滑动窗口注意力模型，再用强化学习进行策略适配，以在保持线性复杂度效率的同时恢复数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理需求快速增长，但标准自注意力随上下文长度呈二次复杂度，推理成本高。滑动窗口注意力更高效，但直接转换和监督微调后在数学推理上仍落后于全注意力模型，因此需要一种实用适配方案。

Method: 方法包含两阶段：第一阶段从预训练 SA 模型出发，通过 SFT 转换为 SWA 模型，避免重新预训练基础模型；第二阶段使用 on-policy 强化学习，使模型在 SWA 约束下基于自身生成轨迹进行策略适配，从而缓解 SFT 数据与 SWA 架构之间的长程依赖不匹配。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，SWARR 显著缩小 SWA 与 SA 的性能差距，恢复了 SWA 转换过程中损失的大部分准确率，同时保留线性复杂度注意力带来的效率优势。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：仅依据转换和 SFT 的结果会低估 SWA 在数学推理中的可行性；引入 RL 后，SWA 模型能够更好适配自身架构约束，显示出兼顾效率与推理性能的潜力。

Abstract: The rapid progress of reasoning and agentic large language models (LLMs) has increased the demand for long-context inference, but self-attention (SA) scales quadratically with context length.
  To address this, we study SWARR (Sliding-Window Attention with Reinforced Adaptation for Math Reasoning), a practical recipe for adapting SWA models to mathematical reasoning. SWARR has two stages: (1) efficient conversion from a pretrained SA model to SWA with supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which avoids pretraining a new base model, and (2) policy adaptation with reinforcement learning (RL).
  We find that SWA still underperforms SA after SFT, and we hypothesize that this gap is caused in part by a data-architecture mismatch: most SFT data are prepared for SA models and may contain long-range dependencies that are difficult for SWA to model. Because on-policy RL optimizes self-generated trajectories under the SWA constraint, it can adapt trajectories to better match SWA.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that this recipe substantially narrows the gap between SWA and SA, recovering much of the accuracy lost during SWA conversion while preserving the efficiency benefits of linear-complexity attention. Our central contribution is the empirical finding that RL changes the conclusion one would draw from conversion and SFT alone about SWA's viability for math reasoning.

</details>


### [132] [TouchThinker: Scaling Tactile Commonsense Reasoning to the Open World with Large-scale Data and Action-aware Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11637)
*Kailin Lyu,Di Wu,Pengwei Zhang,Yuhang Zheng,Yingxin Lai,Long Xiao,Kangyi Wu,Pengna Li,Chen Gao,Lianyu Hu,Xiaobin Hu,Jie Hao,Ce Hao,Weihao Yuan,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TouchThinker 通过百万级多源触觉推理数据集与动作感知表征机制，提升开放世界触觉常识推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉语言系统在开放世界扩展中受限：触觉推理数据规模和形式不足，难以学习可迁移的触觉常识；同时触觉信号冗余且与动作强相关，既有方法忽视这些特性，导致表征低效、语义表达不足。

Method: 提出 TouchThinker 框架：构建 TouchThinker-1M 百万级多源触觉推理数据集，覆盖415个物体、8类场景和7种传感器；建立 TouchThinker-Bench 开放世界评测基准；设计动作感知建模机制，以提高触觉表征效率并支持高效推理。

Result: 实验显示 TouchThinker 在多个数据集上相较现有先进模型取得有竞争力的性能，证明其数据扩展和动作感知表征对触觉常识推理有效。

Conclusion: 该工作从数据和表征两个层面推动触觉-语言推理走向开放世界，为具身智能体理解物理世界提供了更大规模的数据基础和更高效的建模方案。

Abstract: Touch is a key modality for embodied agents to understand the physical world. Although recent work has incorporated tactile signals into language systems for tactile commonsense reasoning, scaling such systems to realistic open-world settings remains challenging due to two key bottlenecks: (1) current tactile reasoning datasets remain limited in format and scale, providing insufficient supervision for reasoning from tactile observations to physical commonsense and hindering the learning of transferable tactile commonsense; (2) Tactile signals are inherently redundant and action-specific, yet existing methods often overlook these properties, resulting in inefficient representations with limited semantic expressiveness. To address these limitations, we propose TouchThinker, a tactile-language framework that scales tactile commonsense reasoning to the open world from both data and representation perspectives. First, we construct TouchThinker-1M, a million-scale, multi-source tactile reasoning dataset covering \textbf{415} objects, \textbf{8} scenarios, and \textbf{7} sensor types, providing a solid data foundation for open-world generalization. We further introduce TouchThinker-Bench, an open-world benchmark with more realistic and diverse tasks. Then, we propose action-aware modeling mechanism to improve tactile representation efficiency and enable efficient reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that TouchThinker achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models across multiple datasets. Our code and dataset will be made available at: https://github.com/lvkailin0118/TouchThinker.

</details>


### [133] [TreeSeeker: Tree-Structured Trial, Error, and Return in Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11662)
*Zhuofan Shi,Mingzhe Ma,Lu Wang,Fangkai Yang,Pu Zhao,Yiming Guan,Youling Huang,Wei Zhang,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang,Saravan Rajmohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TreeSeeker 是一种面向深度搜索任务的推理时框架，通过树状分支、返回与剪枝机制，在多步网页搜索中更有纪律地进行试错，从而提升复杂问题回答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索需要智能体在多个可能方向中进行搜索、浏览、证据比较与综合。核心难点在于：当前看似最优的方向未必最终产生可靠证据；贪婪搜索可能持续扩展薄弱路径，而无约束探索又会浪费预算。

Method: 论文提出 TreeSeeker，将搜索组织为树结构状态上的 branch-and-return 搜索。每个分支表示某个子目标的候选方向。TreeSearch 在每轮读取所有子目标树，识别活跃目标，并基于文本化 UCB 信号（价值、不确定性、风险）在利用高潜力分支、探索不确定替代路径、剪枝无效延续并回到早期分叉点之间做选择。TreeMem 负责将证据、不确定性、冲突、进展和失败线索绑定到产生它们的分支上，以便后续决策利用历史试错结果。

Result: 在 XBench-DeepSearch、BrowseComp 和 BrowseComp-ZH 上，TreeSeeker 持续优于强开源基线，显示其在复杂深度搜索任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式的分支—返回式搜索控制能够补充并增强推理与工具调用能力，使智能体在复杂信息检索与综合任务中更稳健地探索、比较和利用证据。

Abstract: Deep search requires agents to answer complex questions through multi-step web search, browsing, evidence comparison, and synthesis. A central challenge is deciding how to search when several directions look plausible but only some will later lead to reliable evidence. If an agent greedily follows the current best-looking direction, it may keep extending a weak continuation. If it explores without discipline, it may waste budget on disconnected trials. We propose TreeSeeker, an inference-time framework for controlled trial-and-error in deep search. TreeSeeker organizes search as branch-and-return search over tree-structured states, where each branch is a tentative direction for a sub-goal. At each round, TreeSearch reads all sub-goal trees, identifies active goals, and uses textual UCB signals of value, uncertainty, and risk to select among exploiting a promising branch, exploring an uncertain alternative, or pruning an unproductive continuation and returning to an earlier branch point. TreeMem supports this control loop by keeping evidence, uncertainty, conflicts, progress, and failure cues attached to the branches that produced them, so trial outcomes can guide later decisions. Experiments on XBench-DeepSearch, BrowseComp, and BrowseComp-ZH show that TreeSeeker consistently outperforms strong open-source baselines, suggesting that explicit branch-and-return control complements stronger reasoning and tool execution.

</details>


### [134] [Lung-R1: A Knowledge Graph-Guided LLM for Pulmonary Diagnostic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11675)
*Haoyang Zeng,Yuanxi Fu,Rongzhen Li,Yuming Yang,Xiao Sun,Jingwang Huang,Gujie Shao,Guohui Xiang,Quan Lu,Dongfan Ye,Xuetao Chen,Jiang Zhong,Kaiwen Wei,Zhi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LungKG 与 Lung-R1，用结构化肺部知识图谱引导大语言模型进行基于电子病历证据的肺部诊断推理，并在多项评测中取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在肺部医学知识问答和信息处理上已有进展，但真实诊断需要结合患者特异性电子病历证据，处理表型差异与疾病间重叠，并进行关系感知推理。作者将知识问答能力与病例级诊断推理能力之间的差距定义为“肺部知识到诊断差距”。

Method: 作者构建 LungKG，这是一个结构化肺部知识图谱，包含 59,038 个节点、164,308 条边、15 类实体和 112 类关系。基于该知识图谱，提出 Lung-R1：通过知识图谱约束的推理链构建和知识图谱引导的强化学习，对肺部领域大语言模型进行适配训练。

Result: 在包含 20 个系统的评测中，Lung-R1-14B 在 Choice、Pulmonary-QA 和 EMR Diagnosis 三类任务上达到 state-of-the-art。其 EMR Diagnosis 得分为 4.3583，比最强的非 Lung-R1 基线高 0.1476 分。

Conclusion: 实验表明，利用结构化肺部知识图谱引导模型训练，有助于提升大语言模型在电子病历驱动的肺部诊断推理任务中的可靠性和性能。

Abstract: Diagnosing pulmonary diseases requires integrating heterogeneous evidence amid phenotypic variability and cross-disease overlap. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown progress on pulmonary knowledge question answering (QA) and information-processing tasks, reliable pulmonary diagnosis requires patient-specific, relation-aware reasoning over electronic medical record (EMR) evidence rather than isolated knowledge recall. We define this gap between pulmonary knowledge and case-level diagnostic reasoning as the Pulmonary Knowledge-to-Diagnosis Gap. To address it, we introduce LungKG, the first structured pulmonary knowledge graph for diagnostic knowledge organization and record-grounded reasoning. LungKG contains 59,038 nodes and 164,308 edges across 15 entity types and 112 relation types, serving as both a reusable pulmonary knowledge resource and the foundation for LungKG-guided model adaptation. Built on LungKG, we propose Lung-R1, a LungKG-guided pulmonary LLM trained through KG-constrained reasoning-chain construction and KG-guided reinforcement learning. In a 20-system evaluation, Lung-R1-14B achieves state-of-the-art performance across Choice, Pulmonary-QA, and EMR Diagnosis, reaching an EMR Diagnosis score of 4.3583 and surpassing the strongest non-Lung-R1 baseline by 0.1476 points. These results demonstrate the value of LungKG-guided training for EMR-based pulmonary diagnosis.

</details>


### [135] [Organize then Retrieve: Hierarchical Memory Navigation for Efficient Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11680)
*Hao-Lun Hsu,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Boyi Liu,Zhewei Yao,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HORMA 提出一种层次化组织与导航检索的工作记忆机制，用文件系统式结构连接摘要实体与原始轨迹，在显著降低 token 使用和延迟的同时提升长程任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在长程任务中受限于近似无状态特性，需要把任务相关信息不断塞入上下文，导致推理质量下降、推理成本和延迟上升。现有压缩或相似度检索方法容易丢失细节，且难以建模时间结构与因果依赖。

Method: 提出 HORMA，将工作记忆分为结构化记忆构建与基于导航的检索两阶段。构建阶段把经验组织成类似文件系统的层次结构，将摘要实体链接到对应原始轨迹，并通过区分缺失信息与误导/过载上下文导致的失败来迭代优化结构。检索阶段使用强化学习训练的轻量导航智能体遍历层次结构，选择最小但充分的任务相关上下文。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval 上，HORMA 在受限上下文预算下提升任务性能；在长对话任务中 token 使用量最多仅为基线的 22.17%，并相较现有方法取得更优的效率—性能权衡，同时能泛化到未见任务。

Conclusion: 层次化记忆组织结合导航式检索能够在不丢失原始细节的前提下有效降低上下文负担，是提升 LLM 智能体长程任务能力和执行效率的可行方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents struggle with long-horizon tasks due to their inherent statelessness, requiring all task-relevant information to be encoded in growing input contexts. The resulting degraded reasoning quality, increased inference cost, and higher latency necessitate efficient working memory mechanisms. However, existing approaches either rely on lossy compression or similarity-based retrieval, which often fail to capture temporal structure and causal dependencies required for multi-step agentic tasks. In this work, we present HORMA, a Hierarchical Organize-and-Retrieve Memory Agent that organizes experience into a file-system-like hierarchical structure, where summarized entities are linked to the corresponding raw trajectories, enabling efficient access without losing detailed information. HORMA decomposes working memory into two stages: structured memory construction and navigation-based retrieval. The construction module iteratively refines how experiences are structured by distinguishing between failures caused by missing information and those caused by misleading or overloaded context. The navigation module retrieves task-relevant context by traversing the hierarchy using a lightweight agent trained with reinforcement learning to select minimal yet sufficient context, thereby reducing latency along the critical execution path. Across ALFWorld, LoCoMo, and LongMemEval, HORMA improves task performance under constrained context budgets while requiring at most 22.17% of the baseline token usage in long conversation tasks. Compared to existing methods, it consistently achieves better efficiency-performance trade-offs and generalizes effectively to unseen tasks.

</details>


### [136] [Mind the Perspective: Let's Reason Recursively for Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11724)
*Chao Lei,Guang Hu,Meng Yang,Yanbei Jiang,Nir Lipovetzky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RecToM 是一种推理时框架，通过递归构建角色视角来显式建模嵌套信念，从而提升大语言模型在高阶 Theory of Mind 推理任务上的表现，并在多个基准上达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 的 ToM 推理仍难以处理基于部分、非对称观察形成的信念，尤其是高阶嵌套信念。已有提示方法主要依赖可观察事件过滤或时间信念链，缺乏对“某人认为另一个人相信什么”这类嵌套信念的显式建模。

Method: 论文提出 RecToM，在推理阶段沿问题指定的角色链递归构建每个角色的视角；每一层角色视角由前一层视角派生，将高阶信念问题转化为最终构建视角中的实际世界问题。作者还用 KD45 模态逻辑分析证明该视角构建对应良构的信念模态，而不只是简单事件过滤。

Result: 在 Hi-ToM、Big-ToM 和 FanToM 等 ToM 基准上，跨多个 LLM 主干模型，RecToM 稳定优于近期先进方法并取得 state-of-the-art 表现；其中在 Hi-ToM 上，结合 GPT-5.4 和 Qwen3.5 达到 100% 准确率。

Conclusion: RecToM 表明递归视角构建是提升 LLM 高阶 ToM 推理的有效推理时策略，能够比传统事件过滤或时间链方法更准确地处理嵌套信念，并具备一定形式化逻辑支撑。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) reasoning requires inferring agents' beliefs from partial and asymmetric observations, which remains an open challenge for LLMs. Existing prompting-based approaches improve ToM reasoning through observable-event filtering or temporal belief chains, without explicitly modeling nested beliefs. We introduce RecToM, an inference-time framework for ToM reasoning that models nested beliefs via recursive perspective construction. RecToM constructs each character perspective from the preceding character perspective along the character chain specified by the question, reducing higher-order belief questions to actual-world questions within the final constructed perspective. We further provide a KD45 analysis showing that RecToM's perspective construction induces a well-formed belief modality beyond simple event filtering. Experiments on ToM benchmarks, including Hi-ToM, Big-ToM, and FanToM, across multiple LLM backbones show that RecToM consistently outperforms recent advanced approaches, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Notably, RecToM reaches 100\% accuracy on Hi-ToM with GPT-5.4 and Qwen3.5, a benchmark requiring higher-order ToM reasoning.

</details>


### [137] [When Do Data-Driven Systems Exhibit the Capability to Infer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11769)
*Maximilian Poretschkin,Tabea Naeven*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于判定数据驱动系统“推断能力”层级的分析框架，并以信用评分流程说明在欧盟《AI法案》语境下哪些环节可能构成AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟《AI法案》将“具备推断能力”作为AI系统的重要判别特征，但并未清晰界定“推断”的含义，导致信用评分等统计建模系统是否受AI定义约束存在灰色地带。

Method: 作者基于统计学习理论，构建推断能力分级框架；结合《AI法案》及欧盟委员会关于AI系统定义的指南，分析不同层级是否满足法律意义上的推断能力；并设计两个现实化信用评分工作流进行案例说明。

Result: 研究表明，推断能力不应只从单个模型判断，而应考察完整的数据处理工作流；不同建模与人工参与方式会显著影响系统是否以及在何处发生推断。

Conclusion: 该框架有助于澄清信用评分等统计系统在AI监管中的归类问题，但仍存在需要进一步法规明确的边界情形，尤其是人工专家参与和工作流整体性对推断能力判定的影响。

Abstract: The European AI Act is the first comprehensive regulation of artificial intelligence (AI), setting out extensive obligations, particularly for so-called high-risk and general-purpose AI systems. A key distinguishing feature of AI systems under the AI Act is the capability to infer. Since the AI Act does not clearly define what inference is, there is a gray area for certain data-driven systems. A specific example is credit scoring systems, which are listed by Annex III of the AI Act. At the same time, however, these are often implemented using statistical models for which it is unclear whether they have the capability to infer and thus fall under the AI definition of the AI Act at all.
  Motivated by statistical learning theory, this work develops a framework for grading different levels of the capability to infer. Based on the AI Act and the Commission Guidelines on the definition of an artificial intelligence system, we analyze which levels constitute sufficient capability to infer within the meaning of the AI Act and where further regulatory clarity is needed. We illustrate the framework by creating two realistic credit scoring workflows and show whether and where inference occurs in them. Our analysis illustrates that not only individual models but the entire data processing workflow must be considered. It also shows that the involvement of human experts during development can have significant influence on the capability to infer. Code can be found at https://github.com/fraunhofer-iais/inference-framework-creditscorecards.

</details>


### [138] [SVoT: State-aware Visualization-of-Thought for Spatial Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11770)
*Chao Lei,Yanbei Jiang,Markus Hiller,Zhijian Zhou,Xunye Tian,Krista A. Ehinger,Nir Lipovetzky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SVoT，一种用于提升多模态大语言模型多步空间推理可靠性的强化学习框架，通过生成可验证的中间状态与可视化推理，并在新构建的复杂空间推理域上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在空间推理中难以可靠完成跨中间状态和状态转移的多跳推理；已有方法通常不验证中间状态，并将状态转移视为隐式过程，导致推理可靠性不足。同时，现有基准往往把状态转移简化为单变量更新，无法充分评估复杂多对象交互与数值推理能力。

Method: 作者提出 State-aware Visualization-of-Thought（SVoT）强化学习框架，生成交错的文本推理、可视化内容和可验证中间状态；将状态转移推理链显式融入生成过程，使模型能够验证动作前提与动作效果。训练上采用 Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO），通过奖励设计实现细粒度验证，并比较不同奖励形式的效果。

Result: 作者构建了五个评测域，包括扩展经典环境以及新增 Pacman 和 Gather 两个领域，用于评估多对象交互和数值推理下的多跳空间推理。实验显示，采用 transition-aware supervision 的 SVoT 在这些领域达到最优性能，并在分布外测试集上最高获得 65% 的绝对准确率提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模并验证中间状态与状态转移，有助于显著提升 MLLM 在复杂多跳空间推理任务中的可靠性和泛化能力；SVoT 为可验证的文本-视觉交错推理提供了一种有效框架。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning remains a challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), as it requires reliable multi-hop inference over both intermediate states and state transitions. Current studies often leave intermediate states unverified and treat state transitions as implicit processes, which limits reliability in multi-hop spatial reasoning. To address this, we propose State-aware Visualization-of-Thought (SVoT), a reinforcement learning framework that generates interleaved, verifiable intermediate states and visualizations. SVoT integrates transition reasoning chains into the generation processes, enabling the model to verify action preconditions and effects through interleaved textual and visual reasoning. We train SVoT via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), instantiating verification through reward design and evaluating the efficacy of different fine-grained rewards. As existing benchmarks reduce state transitions to single-variable updates, substantially simplifying the problems, we establish five domains by extending classical environments and introducing two novel domains, Pacman and Gather, that require multi-object interactions and numerical reasoning. These domains support systematic evaluation of multi-hop spatial reasoning with quantitative verification of generated intermediate states and transition reasoning. SVoT with transition-aware supervision achieves state-of-the-art performance across the introduced domains, yielding up to a 65% absolute accuracy gain on out-of-distribution test sets.

</details>


### [139] [Toward Trustworthy AI: Multi-Target Adversarial Attacks and Robust Defenses for Continuous Data Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11804)
*Yuefang Lian,Longkun Guo,Zhongrui Zhao,Zhigang Lu,Yanan Cai,Shuchao Pang,Dachuan Xu,Jason Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究连续数据摘要中的相似度级对抗扰动，提出攻击与防御的优化建模及带理论保证的近似算法，并在实验中验证其有效性与鲁棒性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信 AI 不仅依赖下游预测模型的鲁棒性，也依赖上游数据处理流程的可靠性。数据摘要决定哪些信息被保留并传递给后续模块，因此若摘要过程受到对抗扰动，可能降低摘要代表性并损害下游学习任务性能。

Method: 作者将一类多分辨率图像摘要目标表述为非负子模集合函数的多线性扩展，并证明其满足 DR-子模性与 m-弱单调性；进一步将多目标攻击生成为相似度结构扰动下的 min-max 问题，将混合攻击类型下的鲁棒防御表述为正则化 max-min 问题，并分别设计具有近似保证的算法。

Result: 真实数据和受控聚类基准上的实验表明，所提出攻击在低到中等预算条件下能够有效降低摘要质量，并导致下游任务性能损失；所提出防御在结构化场景中改善了鲁棒性与缓解效果之间的权衡，同时揭示了真实数据上鲁棒保护的参数敏感性。

Conclusion: 论文表明，上游数据摘要环节是可信 AI 的潜在脆弱点；通过 DR-子模优化框架可以系统刻画相似度扰动下的攻击与防御，并在理论与实验层面支持其有效性。

Abstract: Trustworthy AI requires reliable data-processing pipelines, not only robust downstream predictive models. As an upstream component, data summarization determines which information is retained and passed to subsequent learning or decision modules. Therefore, adversarial perturbations to the summarization process can compromise trustworthy AI in an upstream manner: they may alter the selected summary, reduce its representativeness, and further degrade the utility of subsequent learning tasks. In this paper, we study adversarial attacks on continuous data summarization under similarity-level perturbations through DR-submodular optimization. We show that a class of multi-resolution image summarization objectives can be formulated as multilinear extensions of non-negative submodular set functions and satisfy DR-submodularity with $m$-weak monotonicity. We then formulate multi-target attack generation as a min-max problem, where one admissible perturbation of the similarity structure is optimized to degrade multiple target summarization models. To mitigate such perturbations, we formulate robust defense against mixed attack types as a regularized max-min problem. For both problems, we develop approximation algorithms with theoretical guarantees. Experiments on real-data and controlled clustered benchmarks show that the proposed attack is effective in representative low-to-moderate budget regimes and can induce downstream task-performance loss. The proposed defense improves the robustness--mitigation trade-off in structured settings, while also revealing the parameter sensitivity of robust protection on real data.

</details>


### [140] [Skill-Augmented AI Agents for Medical Research Analysis: An Exploratory Multi-Model Human Evaluation in an NSCLC Transcriptomic Biomarker Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11830)
*Qianyu Yao,Fei Sun,Bocheng Huang,Wei Chen,Jiarui Jiang,Shu Quan,Yifei Chen,Wenjie Xu,Bo li,Liping Su,Ruoqiong Wu,Huhai Hong,Huimei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探索性评估了AI代理自主调用医学研究技能包是否能提升转录组学研究分析输出质量；结果显示技能增强输出质量评分方向性更高，但差异不显著且受专家评分噪声影响较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型和AI代理正被用于生物医学研究支持，但原生模型输出可能遗漏关键分析步骤、误用方法或夸大结论，因此需要评估为AI代理提供专门医学研究技能包是否能改善分析质量。

Method: 研究以非小细胞肺癌免疫治疗生物标志物任务为场景，测试6种模型骨干，共纳入21份匿名输出，其中9份为原生AI输出、12份为通过OpenClaw代表的AI代理生成的技能增强输出。4名非专家生物医学评审和2名盲法专家对输出进行评价，主要终点为专家评定的总体质量。

Result: 技能增强输出的专家总体质量评分高于原生AI输出，均值为5.50对5.11，差值0.39，bootstrap 95%置信区间为-0.04至0.90，Welch检验p=0.156。非专家评分也呈相同方向，均值为4.72对4.47，差值0.26，95%置信区间为-0.25至0.80，p=0.373。专家一致性较低，单评分ICC为-0.15，模型特异性效应呈描述性且异质。

Conclusion: 自主访问技能包可能带来一定质量提升信号，但在该探索性小样本中证据不足，且提升幅度小于专家评分噪声，不能作为确证性结论。研究主要提示未来需要更大规模、更高可靠性控制、跨平台复现以及生物学有效性评估。

Abstract: Background. Large language models and AI agents are increasingly used to support biomedical research, but native model outputs may omit key analytical steps, misuse methods, or overstate conclusions. We evaluated whether autonomous access to a medical research skill package was associated with higher-quality AI-generated transcriptomic research-analysis outputs compared with native AI without skills. Methods. We conducted an exploratory multi-model human evaluation using a non-small cell lung cancer immunotherapy biomarker task. Six model backbones were tested. The evaluation included 21 anonymized outputs: 9 native-AI outputs and 12 skill-augmented outputs generated through an AI agent implementation represented by OpenClaw. Four non-expert biomedical reviewers and two blinded experts evaluated each output, with two ratings from each reviewer type. The primary outcome was expert-rated overall quality. Results. Skill-augmented outputs showed directionally higher expert overall quality than native-AI outputs (mean 5.50 vs 5.11; difference=0.39; bootstrap 95\% CI, -0.04 to 0.90; Welch p=0.156). Non-expert reviewer quality showed the same direction (mean 4.72 vs 4.47; difference=0.26; bootstrap 95\% CI, -0.25 to 0.80; Welch p=0.373). Expert agreement was limited (single-rating ICC=-0.15), and model-specific effects were descriptive and heterogeneous. Conclusions. Autonomous skill access showed a directional quality signal in this exploratory sample, but the signal was smaller than expert-rating noise and should not be interpreted as confirmatory evidence. The findings primarily motivate larger evaluations of skill-augmented AI agents with stronger reliability controls, platform replication, and biological-validity assessment.

</details>


### [141] [StatefulDiscovery: Evidence-Calibrated Claim Formation in Open-Ended Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11851)
*Jiayao Chen,Shi Liu,Linyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 StatefulDiscovery，一个面向开放式科学发现的框架，通过显式维护“发现状态”来协调探索方向、证据获取与主张判定，从而生成更多高价值且证据充分支持的科学主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式科学发现不同于回答预设问题，智能体需要在多轮探索中自主决定哪些现象值得研究，同时避免过度解读。核心挑战在于证据校准：探索过程必须与主张状态相耦合，使证据既能指导下一步研究，也能限定可合理提出的结论。

Method: 作者提出 StatefulDiscovery，将调查状态外部化，并用该状态协调三个环节：前沿选择、证据获取和主张裁决。框架包含结构化假设、局部裁决和前沿控制等机制，以跟踪证据与主张之间的关系。

Result: 在40个真实数据发现任务上的评估显示，相比多个基线方法，StatefulDiscovery 产生了更多同时被判定为证据充分支持且具有高价值的主张。消融实验表明，结构化假设、局部裁决和前沿控制均对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: 研究表明，显式的发现状态能够有效耦合探索过程与证据校准的主张形成，有助于开放式科学发现智能体在多轮探索中做出更可靠、更有价值的发现。

Abstract: Open-ended scientific discovery asks agents to move beyond executing analyses for predefined questions. Across multiple rounds of exploration, a discovery agent must decide which phenomena warrant investigation while avoiding overinterpretation, where emerging claims exceed the evidential scope of the analyses supporting them. This creates an evidence-calibration problem: the exploration trajectory must be coupled with claim status so that evidence can guide both what to investigate next and what can be claimed. We introduce StatefulDiscovery, a discovery framework that externalizes investigation state and uses it to coordinate frontier selection, evidence acquisition, and claim adjudication. We evaluate StatefulDiscovery across 40 real-data discovery tasks. Compared with several baselines, StatefulDiscovery produces more claims overall judged to be both well-supported and high-value. Ablations indicate that structured hypotheses, local adjudication, and frontier control contribute to performance. Together, these results suggest that explicit discovery state can couple exploration with evidence-calibrated claim formation.

</details>


### [142] [AutoMine Solution for AV2 2026 Scenario Mining Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11874)
*Songliang Cao,Jiele Zhao,Yuru Wang,Hao Li,Daqi Liu,Zehan Zhang,Fangzhen Li,Yu Wang,Yue Zhang,Bing Wang,Guang Chen,Hao Lu,Hangjun Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoMine 是一种结合 LLM 与 VLM 的自动驾驶场景挖掘方法，用于从大规模驾驶日志中稳定发现高价值、安全关键且与规划相关的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶评测依赖数据驱动方法，而大规模驾驶日志中需要自动挖掘安全关键、规划相关的高价值场景；传统方法可能受提示敏感性、感知噪声和开放世界视觉线索处理能力限制。

Method: 提出 AutoMine：通过语义保持的提示增强降低 LLM 对 prompt 的敏感性；结合鲁棒轨迹原子函数与基于 VLM 的函数，以应对感知噪声和开放世界视觉信息；利用真实日志执行反馈对生成代码进行自我精炼。

Result: 在 CVPR 2026 Argoverse 2 Scenario Mining Competition 中，AutoMine 获得 HOTA-Temporal 36.38、Timestamp BA 77.21 的成绩。

Conclusion: AutoMine 展示了 LLM/VLM 结合执行反馈进行自我优化的场景挖掘能力，能够提升自动驾驶日志中关键场景发现的鲁棒性与实用性。

Abstract: With the development of autonomous driving systems, mining high-value, safety-critical, and planning-relevant scenarios from large-scale driving logs has become essential for data-driven evaluation. In this paper, we propose AutoMine, a robust self-refining scenario mining method based on LLMs and VLMs. AutoMine uses semantics-preserving prompt augmentation to reduce LLM prompt sensitivity, combines robust trajectory atomic functions with VLM-based functions to handle perception noise and open-world visual cues, and refines generated code through execution feedback from real logs. In the Argoverse 2 Scenario Mining Competition at CVPR 2026, AutoMine achieves a HOTA-Temporal score of 36.38 and a Timestamp BA score of 77.21.

</details>


### [143] [Embodied-BenchClaw: An Autonomous Multi-Agent System for Embodied Spatial Intelligence Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11909)
*Baoyang Jiang,Fengchun Zhang,Leyuan Wang,Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Zhe Ji,Jinshan Lai,Xi Ren,Jianwei Hu,Qiang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Embodied-BenchClaw，一个可根据用户评测意图自动构建、更新和评估具身空间智能基准的多智能体系统，以降低人工成本并提升基准的可维护性与诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身空间智能基准构建依赖大量人工，复用和维护困难，且多为静态基准，随着模型能力提升容易饱和，难以持续区分新能力。

Method: 系统采用五阶段流水线：意图蓝图生成、数据收集、结构化与清洗、基准合成、评估报告；由规划、构建和评估三个智能体协同完成。同时引入可扩展技能库与过程质量控制，使基准构建具备可组合、可验证和可修复特性。

Result: 作者实例化了覆盖室内/室外空间推理、机器人操作、四足机器人导航、无人机/航拍视角理解和静态基准增强等多个基准。通过人工评估、裁判模型评估、一致性检查、成本分析和消融实验，验证系统可生成可执行、可维护且具有诊断意义的基准，并减少人工工作量。

Conclusion: Embodied-BenchClaw 为具身空间智能评测提供了一种自动化、可扩展、可持续更新的基准构建范式，有助于缓解静态基准饱和和维护成本高的问题。

Abstract: Benchmarks are essential for evaluating embodied spatial intelligence, yet their construction is labor-intensive, hard to reuse, and difficult to maintain. Existing embodied benchmarks are often static and may quickly become saturated as models improve, limiting their ability to distinguish new capabilities. We propose Embodied-BenchClaw, an autonomous agentic system for constructing embodied spatial intelligence benchmarks.
